# DECISIONS.md — PathPilot AI Day 1

| # | Decision | Rationale |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Day 1 backend only; no frontend, no GitHub push (original scope) | Task scope: another agent owns `frontend/`; no push requested for Day 1. (Superseded 2026-11-24: frontend integrated and Day 1 pushed — see #18.) |
| 2 | Two independent tracks (undergraduate / taught_masters), cross-track profiles rejected with 422 `TRACK_MISMATCH` | Competition product design; prevents meaningless cross-track matches. |
| 3 | Only 2 real programmes in v1, each fact traced to a source id + excerpt | No fabricated data; Day-1 scope is a working rules engine, not coverage. |
| 4 | Rules/template explain mode with no AI key; `ai_status=unavailable` | No cloud AI key available and none required by the competition; the explain endpoint must work without one. |
| 5 | ProviderAdapter reads only env vars (`AI_PROVIDER`, `AI_MODEL`, `AI_API_KEY`); no key in code | Secrets rule; `.env.example` holds placeholders only. |
| 6 | UNKNOWN, never NOT_MET, for: UCL TOEFL, UCL TARA, UCL non-A-level/IB qualifications, NUS missing/low GRE/GMAT, mismatched/missing budget | The verified sources state no threshold or no rejection rule; converting or rejecting would invent evidence. (Corrected 2026-11-24 audit: NUS IELTS components are NOT_APPLICABLE, not UNKNOWN — see #21; the UCL TOEFL reason was corrected — see #22; the NUS GRE/GMAT reason was corrected — see #23.) |
| 7 | NUS application deadline recorded as UNKNOWN (null) | Not stated on the admissions page; General Track dates deliberately not copied. |
| 8 | UCL BSc page recorded as `fetch_status: failed` (direct curl exit 18), facts from browser crawl | Honest provenance; no hash claimed for that page. |
| 9 | Fixture `fixture-p01` is synthetic, lives only in `data/fixtures/`, and the backend refuses fixtures in real data at startup | Test isolation without contaminating real data. |
| 10 | Overall status: any NOT_MET → NOT_MET; else any UNKNOWN → UNKNOWN; else KNOWN_MINIMUMS_MET | Deterministic summary rule shared by engine and tests. |
| 11 | CORS limited to `http://localhost:3000` | Frontend dev origin only. |
| 12 | Backend runs on port 8000, frontend on port 3000 | Convention agreed for the two-agent split. |
| 13 | Python 3.12 venv in the project (`.venv/`), pinned requirements in `backend/requirements.txt` | Reproducible local run; no paid resources. |
| 14 | Backend accepts BOTH API shapes: spec/test contract (single `program_id`, nested StudentProfile) and frontend contract (flat wizard profile + `program_ids`, multi-program response) | The two Day-1 agents built against different shapes; the adapter translates without touching rule logic. Both shapes are pinned by tests. |
| 15 | Adapter maps engine evidence ids (manifest fact ids) to frontend source ids | The frontend evidence drawer looks sources up by its own ids; unmapped ids would show an empty drawer. |
| 16 | Wizard budget assumed per-year for undergraduate, total for taught masters | Matches the wizard (currency defaults GBP for UG, SGD for masters); recorded as an assumption, not a verified fact. |
| 17 | Frontend source URLs fixed to the verified pages (NUS fees `/pg/mcs/scholarships/`, UCL `-2026` page, UCL English Level 1 page) and the A-level excerpt corrected to the page wording | The frontend draft used `/pg/mis/scholarships/` (typo, not the verified page) and a no-year UCL URL (403 via curl); the A-level excerpt invented an "A*AA" alternative the page does not state. |
| 18 | Day 1 scope grew: frontend integrated + GitHub push completed 2026-11-24 (HEAD ccf8aba, 14 commits, public repo) | Day 1 had been presented as done while the repo was still empty; the user selected PathPilot-AI in the GitHub App installation and the push was completed the same day. The audit fixes of 2026-11-24 still need a push. |
| 19 | UCL contextual offer (A*AB with A* in Maths; IB 38) → UNKNOWN, not NOT_MET | The official page lists the contextual offer as a real alternative route; marking it a failure invents a rejection. Without evidence of contextual eligibility it cannot be MET either. (Audit fix, tests added.) |
| 20 | NUS related-discipline route requires honours; a related bachelor's + 2y experience → UNKNOWN, not MET | The old engine branch contradicted the official four routes and produced a false MET — the spec treats a false MET as a release blocker. Business and computing bachelor's + 2y remain MET (listed routes). (Audit fix, tests added.) |
| 21 | NUS IELTS components → NOT_APPLICABLE, not UNKNOWN | No component minimum exists, so there is no component requirement to check; UNKNOWN made KNOWN_MINIMUMS_MET unreachable for every IELTS NUS applicant. (Audit fix, contract test updated.) |
| 22 | UCL TOEFL: a Level 1 mapping DOES exist on the cited Study Abroad English page (92 old iBT scale; 4.5 new scale for tests from 21 Jan 2026); keep UNKNOWN for now, never convert | The Day 1 claim that "no TOEFL threshold exists in the verified sources" was wrong — the cited page lists one. Converting from the wrong page/scale would be worse than UNKNOWN; modelling it needs a test date (Day 2, see #30). Wording corrected in the engine, manifest, and QUALITY_REPORT. |
| 23 | NUS GRE/GMAT "FAQ: not mandatory" citation removed; UNKNOWN stands on the verifiable reason | The fetched admissions page does not contain that FAQ statement. The page lists GRE/GMAT as evidence to submit but states no rejection rule for a missing/low score — that alone justifies UNKNOWN. Wording corrected in the engine, programs.json, manifest, QUALITY_REPORT, and adapter. |
| 24 | Frontend DATA_VERSION aligned to `v1` (was `pathpilot-day1-2026-11-24`) | One version string for one dataset; the backend ignores the frontend-sent version, so the mismatch was silent drift. (Audit fix.) |
| 25 | Offline evaluator aligned to the backend: GRE/GMAT below minimum → UNKNOWN (was NOT_MET); UCL TARA rule added; NUS components rule added; rule ids renamed to backend ids (ucl-fee, nus-fee, nus-academic, nus-language) | The fallback must not contradict live results, or the UI would show different verdicts depending on backend availability. Remaining offline gaps (no honours/experience fields in the flat profile) are Day 2 work. (Audit fix; verified by `npm run build`.) |
| 26 | POST /explain must recompute server-side (spec Ch12), not accept a client-supplied Evaluation | Accepting forged results lets a caller claim KNOWN_MINIMUMS_MET (demonstrated live in the audit). Not changed on Day 1 — the endpoint is unused by the frontend, and changing its shape is a Day 2 API decision. (BLOCKERS #8, TASKS D2-04.) |
| 27 | Default language stays en for now — user decision, not changed | Spec FR11 order is saved → browser → en, but the user has not answered whether the default should be en or zh-CN; changing it unilaterally would override an open question. Day 2: implement browser-language detection on first visit once the default is decided. |
| 28 | Spec Ch18 is a 5-day plan; the user's 3-day plan (Day 1 Nov 24, Day 2 Nov 25, Day 3 Nov 26, buffer to the Nov 28 deadline) is better | Leaves 2 buffer days before the 2026-11-28 15:00 PST deadline. Already the working plan; recorded here. |
| 29 | Spec Ch12 fixture example is test-harness only: fixture-p01 is not served by /match (404) and its evidence id is `fixture`, not `fixture-f01` | Ch14 isolation (production must refuse fixtures) outranks the illustrative example; the spec example should be read as a test-harness contract, not an API contract. |
| 30 | Spec Ch11 full StudentProfile model (id, schema_version, target_intake, education[], language_tests[] with dates) vs the Day 1 simplified profile | The spec's model is better for the full product; the Day 1 simplification was the right scope. But FR01 fields (intake year, test date, graduation status) must be added in Day 2 — without a test date, the UCL TOEFL Jan 2026 scale split and score-validity windows (BR02) cannot be implemented. |
| 31 | Money: Python float, not Decimal (spec Ch11 requires Decimal) | The spec is better — money must be exact. Day 1 float is fine for whole-number thresholds but must switch to Decimal before any currency conversion or per-unit maths in Day 2. |
| 32 | No rate limiting, security headers, or OpenAPI (spec NFR04 / Ch12) in Day 1 | The spec is better — add in Day 2 before the public preview link goes live; the preview will be internet-facing. (BLOCKERS #9, TASKS D2-06.) |
| 33 | No traceability.csv (spec Ch16) in Day 1; manifest + QUALITY_REPORT cover the same ground | Generate a traceability.csv in Day 2 derived from the manifest rather than hand-maintaining a third copy. (BLOCKERS #9.) |
| 34 | /programs has no filtering/pagination (spec Ch12: country/subject/intake/cursor/limit, next_cursor); /profile/validate returns only {valid, application_track} (spec: normalized_profile/warnings/field_errors) | The spec is better — needed once Day 2 grows to 8+ courses; acceptable with 2 courses on Day 1. (TASKS D2-06.) |
| 35 | Evidence excerpts are English-only; spec Ch9 requires auxiliary translations + expandable original | The spec is better — Day 2: translate excerpts per locale or label them English-only honestly. |
| 36 | Wizard gaps (FR01/FR12): no IB total / HL Maths input, no IT-experience-years input, no intake year, no test date, no graduation status; adapter prefers IELTS and silently ignores TOEFL if any IELTS field is present | The spec is better — add the fields in Day 2; the engine already supports IB and experience, the wizard just never sends them. (TASKS D2-05.) |
| 37 | UCL fee rule assumes the overseas fee (£46,700); no UK/home-fee path (no residency/domicile input) | Add a residency field in Day 2; until then the fee rule is overseas-only and the wizard should say so. |
| 38 | UCL GCSE English + Maths (grade C/4) required on the official page but not modelled (no GCSE field) | The spec is better — add to the Day 2 wizard/engine; currently a student missing GCSEs could be told the academic rule is MET. |
| 39 | IB "20 points in three HL subjects" not checked (no HL-total field) | Add an HL-total input in Day 2; until then an IB applicant with total 40 but HL sum <20 is wrongly MET. |
| 40 | Rubric: the live rule is 25 checklist items scored per item then averaged and added (Day 1 docs correct; "five checkmarks per item" was from an older version) | Code/docs are better — verified against the live Devpost rules page on Day 1. |

| 41 | First-open default language is ENGLISH — user decision 2026-11-25 00:25 | Saved locale wins, otherwise en. No browser-language detection that would override this. Markdown files stay in English (Devpost requires English). (Day 2 decision.) |
| 42 | Day 2 data version is v2; v1 files stay intact for traceability | New courses go into data/versions/v2/ (programs.json, source-manifest.json, QUALITY_REPORT.md); backend + frontend DATA_VERSION switch to v2 together once v2 is verified. |
| 43 | UCL TOEFL is now modelled (supersedes the "not yet modelled" part of #22): Jan 2026 scale split from the UCL Study Abroad English page, re-checked live 2026-11-25 | Tests from 21 Jan 2026 use the new scale (Level 1 overall 4.5); earlier tests use the old scale (92). No test_date → UNKNOWN, never converted. Separate rule id ucl-toefl, evaluated whenever a TOEFL score is supplied (primary exam, or toefl_overall alongside IELTS). The v1 QUALITY_REPORT wording "not yet modelled" is now stale — the v2 data refresh should update it. |
| 44 | IB HL total is required for MET (fixes #39) | Total 40 + HL Maths 7 with no HL total → UNKNOWN ("HL total not supplied"); HL total < 20 with total 40 + HL Maths 7 → NOT_MET; contextual IB 38 + HL Maths 7 + 18 HL points → UNKNOWN. No existing test expectations changed: no Day 1 test asserted IB MET without an HL total (all 25 Day 1 tests pass unmodified). |
| 45 | GCSE rule cites forward-reference fact id f-ucl-gcse | The GCSE C/4 requirement is stated on the UCL BSc page (source ucl-bsc) but the v1 manifest has no dedicated GCSE fact and v1 is frozen, so the engine cites f-ucl-gcse and the adapter maps it to ucl-cs-entry; the v2 manifest must add the fact. A dangling id is the honest interim — inventing a v1 fact would be worse. |
| 46 | Fee residency: uk → £9,790/yr, overseas or missing → £46,700/yr | Documented assumption (see #37): the rule detail says "residency not supplied, overseas assumed" when residency is missing, and the wizard must say so. The UK fact f-ucl-fee-uk already exists in the v1 manifest. |
| 47 | POST /explain recomputes server-side; response is the /match-style multi-program shape with an explanation per program | Old shape {evaluation} → 422 EXPLAIN_REQUIRES_PROFILE. New shape {profile, program_ids, locale}; profile may be nested or flat. The ExplainRequest model documents the shape, but the endpoint parses raw JSON so the forged shape can be rejected with the specific code instead of FastAPI's generic 422. |
| 48 | Rate limiting is in-memory per-IP, sliding 60 s window: 60/min general, 10/min on /explain (own bucket), /health and /ready exempt | The spec's anonymous-AI-call budget is 10/min/IP (Ch15). reset_rate_limits() is a test-only hook so the shared TestClient IP does not 429 unrelated tests; it is not an endpoint. |
| 49 | Money is Decimal; language scores stay float in Day 2 | Budget.amount is Decimal; JSON stays a plain number via _plain_number/_plain_json because pydantic/FastAPI render Decimal as a string in model_dump(mode="json"). Spec Ch11 also wants Decimal for language scores — not changed here (task scoped Decimal to money); open for Day 3. |
| 50 | OpenAPI lives at both /openapi.json and /api/v1/openapi.json; docs/openapi.json is generated, never hand-edited | tools/generate_openapi.py dumps the live app object; tests/test_day2.py::test_openapi_matches_generated_copy pins the file to the live spec. |
| 51 | /programs keeps stable file order (UCL first), not id-sorted | A Day 1 test pins the order ["ucl-cs-bsc-2026", "nus-mcomp-cs"]; sorting would break it. Cursor is a base64url offset token; limit is clamped to 20 (not an error); an invalid cursor is 400 VALIDATION_ERROR. |
| 52 | /profile/validate returns 200 with {valid, warnings, field_errors} for value problems; track mismatch stays 422 TRACK_MISMATCH | A Day 1 test pins the 422 for cross-track profiles. Grades are never silently rescaled — out-of-range values are reported as field_errors. |
| 53 | Traceability is generated, not hand-maintained | tools/generate_traceability.py derives data/versions/v2/traceability.csv from the v1 manifest (evidence fact ids verified) + TASKS.md (D2-04/D2-06 rows); release_sha is "unknown" here because this workspace has no local git repo — the coordinator re-runs it under git. |
| 54 | First-open default language is ENGLISH — user decision 2026-11-25 (frontend record; same decision as #41) | Saved locale wins, otherwise en. No browser-language detection is implemented (store.tsx default stays 'en'; saved 'pp-locale' is the only override). Markdown files stay in English (Devpost requires English). Supersedes the open question in #27 / D2-07: the default is decided, detection is explicitly not wanted. |

| 55 | v2 data built from the research draft (~/workspace/day2_courses.json): 8 programmes, 72 facts, 29 sources, 25 unknowns; 0 bad cross-references | Draft validated programmatically before splitting into data/versions/v2/programs.json + source-manifest.json; v1 files untouched. Two brief targets were 404s and were switched (Imperial BSc → BEng; NTU CS BEng → BComp CS Hons), recorded as failed sources. |
| 56 | NUS/NTU undergraduate academic rule is always UNKNOWN (IGP is not a threshold) | Neither university publishes a fixed A-level/IB minimum; only Indicative Grade Profiles of admitted students. Treating IGP as a pass/fail threshold would invent a rejection rule. The IGP figures are shown in the rule detail as context only. |
| 57 | UCL MSc CS language is UNKNOWN (no English level stated on the course page) | Third-party reports of Level 2 (IELTS 7.0/6.5) are not official evidence and were not used. The Level 2 definition is recorded as a definition fact only. |
| 58 | Singapore undergraduate fee rule needs a residency band; ambiguous bands are UNKNOWN | Residency values: sg_citizen (S$8,300), sg_pr (S$11,600), sg_intl_asean (S$18,050), sg_intl_other (S$21,400), sg_intl_nogrant (non-subsidised band not labelled for CS → UNKNOWN), uk/overseas without grant status → UNKNOWN. AY2026/27 rates are the latest published. |
| 59 | Imperial MSc academic: First-Class required; computing degree → NOT_MET; non-STEM needs A in A-level Maths | "First-Class degree in a subject without substantial Computing content" is a stated exclusion, so a computing degree is a real NOT_MET. For non-STEM fields (business/other) the A-in-Maths condition needs evidence, else UNKNOWN. Related (STEM) fields are treated as meeting the maths condition — recorded assumption, since the condition is stated for non-STEM applicants. |
| 60 | UCL MSc CS academic: computing/IT degree → NOT_MET; otherwise upper-second+ with maths to A-level standard | The programme requires a degree in a subject OTHER than CS/IT — a stated exclusion. Maths evidence (mastersMathsGrade) is required for MET, else UNKNOWN. |
| 61 | Real AI model call (FR06) is explicitly deferred in Day 2 — rules/template mode, ai_status=unavailable | No provider API key exists and none is required by the competition; no authorisation to buy one or spend money. The explain path is citation-checked (claims without evidence are dropped) and degrades on provider failure, so the product is honest in rules mode. A real model call needs a key the user provides; that is a Day 3 / user decision, not something to fake. |
| 62 | Offline evaluator models the new Day 2 rules (IB HL total, GCSE, TOEFL date, residency fees, new programmes) | The fallback must not contradict live results (DECISIONS #25). New-programme academic rules that depend on IGP-only evidence stay UNKNOWN offline, same as live. |
| 63 | Frontend v2 integration (2026-11-25): 8 programmes in the offline fallback, SG residency + degree class in the wizard, 138 i18n keys | `frontend/src/lib/programs.ts` now carries all 8 v2 entries with official sources/excerpts (verified 2026-11-25); `residency` extended with the 5 SG bands and `degreeClass`/`mastersMathsGrade` added to Profile/emptyProfile; wizard masters step has a degree-class select + A-level Maths grade, residency select has the 5 SG options; messages +12 keys (126 → 138) in all 6 locales. |
| 64 | Day 2 preview deployment is BLOCKED on the user's platform authorisation — app is deploy-ready, not live | Vercel connector reports `not_connected` (`vercel status` 2026-11-25); no Vercel/Railway/Render account or token exists on this machine and none was created. Deploy-ready artifacts: `DEPLOY.md` (exact steps + deploy button), `backend/Dockerfile` (build from repo root), env-driven `API_BASE_URL` in `frontend/next.config.js`, Vercel button in README. The parent must ask Xiangfeng for the Vercel OAuth approval (and a Railway/Render backend host) — no login as the user, no spend. |
| 65 | Day 2 final verification (2026-11-25): 79 tests, build pass, live green | pytest 79 passed 0 failed (25 Day 1 + 6 contract + 36 Day 2 + 12 v2); `npm run build` pass (6 × 138 keys); backend v2 live (8 programmes) + frontend :3000 all 8 pages 200; `/match` Imperial BEng + NTU MSAI verified; `/simulate` delta verified; forged `/explain` → 422; headers present. No Day 1 audit-fix regression. |
| 66 | Curriculum is the FIRST wizard step for everyone (user requirement, fix 2026-11-25) | Six branches: China Gaokao, UK A-level+GCSE, IB, USA SAT/ACT/AP, Singapore A-level, Other. Only the branch's fields are shown; switching curriculum clears the other branches' fields so stale values are never evaluated. For taught masters the curriculum is recorded but does not change evaluation (degree-based). `effective_curriculum` falls back to inference from `qualification_type` for pre-branch API clients, so old behaviour is preserved. |
| 67 | Gaokao → UNKNOWN for UCL CS BSc and Imperial Computing BEng (verified 2026-11-24 17:37 UTC) | UCL China page states UG minimum ABB at A-level or IB 36, plus a one-year bachelor's at 90% as an alternative — no Gaokao direct-entry route. Imperial's accepted-qualifications table has no China row at all. Third-party Gaokao claims (85–90%+, top 0.1% province) are not official evidence and were not used. New facts `f-ucl-china-gaokao`, `f-imperial-china-none` in the v2 manifest; reason key `gaokao-no-equivalence`. |
| 68 | Imperial USA AP standard: route A (five APs 5,5,5,4,4) or route B (three APs 5,5,4 + SAT ≥1460 or ACT ≥31); standard met → UNKNOWN, below standard → NOT_MET | From Imperial's accepted-qualifications page (verified 2026-11-24 17:37 UTC). Standard met only establishes equivalence to 3 A-levels; the course-specific A* in Mathematics cannot be checked from AP grades alone (the page states some courses require higher than the general minimum). Reason key `usa-ap-general-only`. UCL publishes no US equivalence → UNKNOWN `usa-no-equivalence`. |
| 69 | Singapore A-levels are assessed as A-levels | Documented assumption for UCL (same GCE A-level system); verified for Imperial (accepted-qualifications page: three H2 subjects at grade A). SG branch shows A-level + Maths fields but no GCSE fields; GCSE rule is NOT_APPLICABLE for SG. |
| 70 | Reason keys are derived in the adapter from the engine's English detail strings (single place, test-pinned), NOT added to the engine's RuleResult | Avoids touching ~40 `_r()` calls. `reasonKey` (16 values) + `observedRaw`/`thresholdRaw` travel in the API response; the frontend renders rule text from those structured values through the locale catalogue. Backend English `expected`/`observed`/`unknownReason` strings stay as fallbacks only. |
| 71 | Short chip names are per rule family (13 keys), not per rule id | 44 per-rule names would be unreadable and redundant across programmes. Families: academic, ieltsOverall, language, englishLevel, ieltsComponents, toefl, gcse, tara, tmua, admissionsTest, fee, greGmat, gre, writing, rule. Chip text is `{ruleName}{colon}{statusText}` ("学术：符合"). |
| 72 | "Budget GBP 22/year" was the user's own entered budget, not a sample | `sampleUndergrad` budget is 50000 GBP, `sampleMasters` 70000 SGD — both realistic. Only the blocker-line format was wrong, fixed as a sentence via `formatFeeSentence` ("你的预算 GBP 22/年，低于学费 GBP 46,700/年"). No sample changed. |
| 73 | Detail-page minimums come from PROGRAM_RULES (ruleText.ts) in display order; conditional TOEFL rules are listed only where the main language rule is IELTS-only | Where the language rule already states the TOEFL alternative (NUS MComp, NTU MSAI), the supplementary TOEFL rule is left out of the minimums list. The minimums are the localised `rule.<id>.expected` texts — the recorded minimums, labelled "not a result", with a "开始资格检查" button in the section. |
| 74 | First-visit modal: shown only when no evaluation exists and `pp-welcome-dismissed` is unset in localStorage; dismissible by button, backdrop click, or CTA; never reappears once dismissed or once a check exists | Smiley is an inline SVG (not emoji). The line under the button reuses `results.empty` ("还没有资料。请先开始资格检查。"). Motion uses the existing `anim-fade-in` class, which the global `prefers-reduced-motion` rule already neutralises. The check reads `pp-profile` directly in the effect to avoid the store hydration race. |
| 75 | Flow after the check (req. D): a next-steps panel on the results page + a "View report" link in each result card | The panel sits directly under the title/offline notes (above the cards, not buried below the simulation panel), shows counts of meets-all / blockers / unknowns, and links to the full report (`/report`, primary) and back to the wizard to refine the profile. Rationale: the report page already exists as the printable/analysis artifact, so making it the explicit next step gives a clear forward path without inventing a new comparison view (Day 3 FR08 action comparison is separate scope). The report page also gained a "Back to results" link. |
| 76 | Home banner wording: "Showing the built-in verified programme catalogue — the live backend is not connected yet." | Honest and specific: the frontend is showing its built-in verified catalogue because the backend is still undeployed (BLOCKERS #10). Same key `programs.offlineNote`, new values in all 6 locales. |
| 77 | Offline evaluator parity: supplementary TOEFL rules (`nus-toefl`, `ntu-msai-toefl`) added to `evaluateLocal` | The backend evaluates a TOEFL supplied alongside IELTS as its own rule instead of dropping it; the offline evaluator did the same for `ntu-msai-toefl` only — `nus-toefl` was missing and is now added. `ucl-ielts-components` threshold `{each_component: 6.0}` renders all four components (the renderer treats `each_component` as "all four"). |
| 78 | Detail-page back link goes home ("返回首页"), not to results | Same key `program.back`, new value in all 6 locales. Duration, intake label and fee period are localised per programme (`program.duration.<id>`, `program.intake.<id>`, `fee.period.*`); institution and official programme names stay as proper nouns; source labels stay English (part of the source literature). |
| 79 | Evidence excerpts are NOT translated | Only the "Excerpt in English (original)" label is localised (`evidence.englishOnly`); the excerpt text itself keeps its original language. Machine-translating official source text would misrepresent evidence. |
| 80 | Render test caught 3 real bugs before the build (fix 2026-11-25) | (1) `shortKey` missed `ucl-ielts-components` → chip rendered "规则：不符合"; (2) `formatObserved` dispatch missed `-ielts-components` → components observed fell through to the raw-object dump ("listening: 6.5, …"); (3) `rule.ntu-msai-language-components.expected` was missing from the locale catalogue AND from the backend EXPECTED dict. All fixed; the standalone render test (`/tmp/pp-test-src/render-test.ts`, tsc + node with a fake `t()` backed by the real locale files) now passes all checks in zh-CN and en. |

| 81 | Rankings scope (Day 3, 2026-11-25) — superseded by #88 | The original scope was the four programme institutions only; the top-100 expansion on 2026-11-25 superseded it (see #88). The data model keys entries by (provider, institution_id, edition_label) so more institutions can be added later without a model change. |
| 82 | QS 2027 ranks verified from QS's own `.cn` data file; THE 2027 from its JSON feed; ARWU 2026 from its Nuxt payload file (all re-fetched 2026-11-25 ~03:05–03:12 UTC) | The `.com` QS page is Cloudflare-blocked for curl (403) and the THE/ARWU pages are JS-driven; the providers' own data files are the same tables. Values recorded: QS UCL `=8` (96.3), Imperial `=2` (99.2), NUS 10 (96.2), NTU 12 (93.6); THE UCL `=17` (90.3), Imperial 8 (95.2), NUS 15 (91.1), NTU 28 (84.0); ARWU UCL 17 (49.4), Imperial 29 (41.2), NUS 53 (33.3), NTU 84 (28.7). Rank displays kept verbatim (`=` = tie); scores are the providers' published overall scores. |
| 83 | Fourth provider's entries unavailable with an explicit reason — superseded by #89 | That provider's official table could not be opened from this environment, so no rank_display or score was recorded and only the official entry link was shown. The provider was removed entirely on 2026-11-25 (see #89). |
| 84 | Rights are labelled honestly, never presented as licensed (Day 3) | QS `conditional-attribution`: the terms' carve-out permits reproduction as displayed with the exact notice ("Source: QS Quacquarelli Symonds (www.qs.com). With permission. Copyright © QS Quacquarelli Symonds Ltd."), hyperlinks retained, no reconfiguration — the "With permission" phrase is the required notice wording, not an individual approval. THE `permission-not-obtained`: website terms require prior written consent (data@timeshighereducation.com); values are shown only because the user explicitly directed inclusion, and the UI/docs say so. ARWU `terms-unclear`: no terms page exists, "All Rights Reserved", no reuse permission found (contact@shanghairanking.com); same honest labelling. |
| 85 | No combined/average rank across providers anywhere (BR15), enforced by test | Entries carry no combined/average field; the compare table is per provider+edition (rows = providers, columns = institutions), so a world rank is never compared with a national rank and a subject rank is never presented as a course rank. `tests/test_rankings.py::test_no_combined_or_average_rank_anywhere` pins this. |
| 86 | Subject ranks are out of scope for this build; a subject filter returns an empty list | QS subject tables and ARWU GRAS exist but no subject-table access or rights were checked (RANKINGS_SOURCES.md §6); THE subject tables likewise unchecked. Returning empty with an explanation is honest; inventing a subject rank would violate T21. |

| 117 | Testing round 3 fixes (2026-11-25, user screenshots + messages) | (1) Before/after: the slider was pointless ("你做个图片就行了") — deleted `BeforeAfter.tsx` and replaced it with ONE static image per locale (`frontend/public/before-after-*.svg`, 1200×460; white Before panel + navy After panel, amber divider with a static ⇄ medallion, same copy, no programme facts; RTL locales mirror the panels). Only `computing` maps to catalogue programmes today (all 8 v2 programmes are Computer Science); other fields show the honest empty state + a /rankings link. No admission probabilities anywhere. | 118 | Design research round 2 (2026-11-25, user: "设计缺乏高级感，组合太简单，不精密，太单调，而且没用切换动画，而且切换动画应该高级，小罗盘也不会动也不互动"; research in `~/workspace/design_research_2.md` — Obys, Lando Norris, Dropbox Brand, Species in Pieces, Lama Lama, mascot state machines, page-transition taxonomy; nothing copied) | Eight moves, applied in order: (1) One easing family — exactly two curves site-wide (`--ease-enter cubic-bezier(.22,1,.36,1)`, `--ease-reveal cubic-bezier(.16,1,.3,1)`); every entrance animation migrated to them (the pet's pop keeps its overshoot — character motion, not UI motion). One masked line-reveal for all text: headings slide up from translateY(110%) inside an overflow-hidden mask (`.line-mask`, descender padding so glyphs never clip), applied to the hero h1 and every main page/section title. One underline primitive for nav links (amber scaleX mask from the inline-start side; RTL flips the origin). (2) Route transition on the compass motif: `RouteTransition` keyed by pathname in the root layout — full variant (home + wizard) is a thin amber route line drawing itself across the top (LTR left→right, RTL right→left) while the page rises .5s, total ~.55s < 0.8s cap; quiet variant (evidence pages: rankings, institution/programme detail, results, report, cases) is a calm .3s fade only. No preloader, no full-screen cover, no hold — users click constantly. Keyboard focus moves to `<main>` on route change with preventScroll (scroll restoration intact). Header: brand compass quarter-turns on route change (keyed span; inner SVG keeps its sway) + active nav gets the amber underline + aria-current. `.page-enter` removed from page roots, kept for wizard step changes only. (3) 小罗盘 as a state machine: idle / blink (CSS) / think / celebrate / empathise / bored, driven by app events — thinking pulse ring while busy, celebration bounce when a suggestion is found, sympathetic face when offline (the offline flag now resets on the next live success so empathise never sticks), boredom after 60s idle with the panel closed (needle looks around, eyes half-close; any pointer/keyboard activity wakes it); eyes + needle follow the pointer (rAF-throttled, direct DOM writes, ≤400px, skipped under reduced motion); lean-in while the user types. Implementation detail: three nested needle groups (JS steer / bored look / CSS sway) and blink on the eye circles, so no two transforms share one element; the celebrate animation is defined after the bob in the stylesheet so it wins while both classes are present. Policy, questions and answers unchanged. (4) Shared-element transitions: `ViewTransitionLink` navigates inside `document.startViewTransition` on a plain left-click (double-rAF wait so React commits before the snapshot; modifier/middle clicks, reduced motion and no-API browsers fall back to a plain Link); `vtRef` assigns `view-transition-name` via a ref callback (React 18 warns on the style prop); the root cross-fade is disabled in CSS so only the named elements morph, and the route entrance is suppressed for these navigations via a one-shot flag (the view-transition snapshot of the new page is taken while the route entrance is at opacity 0, which would hide the morph). Crest morphs rankings row → institution header (a crest was added to the institution header, monogram fallback included); programme title morphs card / results / institution-list → detail header. Known limit: the App Router's router.push returns no promise, so the double-rAF wait is best-effort — on a slow render the snapshot may still catch the old page and the morph silently does not happen; navigation still works. Verify visually in a real browser. (5) 12-column grid + type scale: hero 7/5, section headers span 8 of 12 (4 deliberate empty cells), programme/case cards and how-it-works steps on `.grid-12` (1 col on phones, 12 from md up); `.section-rule` hairlines between homepage sections; `.card-surface` layered shadow on feature cards; type scale as CSS tokens with the existing classes mapped to them. (6) Amber rationing + motion budget: audit found amber is already the only accent (CTAs, NOW line, verification ring, pet ring, hero gradient); the only other colour is semantic red for form errors — no second accent. Motion is spent on hero, wizard and pet; evidence pages get the quiet route variant and lost their entrance staggers (rankings rows, results cards). (7) Ring + NOW encode real data — verified, no change: the ring's number is the real official-source count (capped at 5 only for the ring scale) and the NOW marker is today's real date on the Dec 2026 → Oct 2027 scale. (8) RTL/keyboard/reduced-motion safety: route line flips in RTL, grid spans mirror automatically, line-reveal is translateY (direction-neutral), nav underline origin flips in RTL, focus moves to main on route change, view transitions are skipped under reduced motion and the route line is display:none there. Real-phone verification is still open. |

| 143 | PrefChat is now model-led (round 7) | `frontend/src/components/PrefChat.tsx` reworked; contract helpers in `frontend/src/lib/chat.ts` (`PrefKnown`, `PrefChatRequest`, `PrefExtracted`, `PrefChatReply`, `knownFromProfile`, `countKnownPrefs`, `firstMissingPref`, `isPrefKnown`, `effectiveNextPref`, `profilePatchFromExtracted`, `extractedSummary`, `isModelPrefReply`). Each user turn → `POST /api/assistant/pref-chat` with `{message, locale, known, history (last 6 turns)}`. The model's lines render as the pet bubble; `extracted` (non-empty only) opens a confirmation bubble built by `extractedSummary` — values in the current locale + the user's own words (city, `note` reason) verbatim and quoted. Yes applies via `profilePatchFromExtracted` (countries merge, never replace; note appends with `；` instead of overwriting); No dismisses. Nothing is applied before confirmation. `next_question` drives the chips; the effective-next rule (`effectiveNextPref`, unit-tested) prefers the server's answer while it is still unanswered, else the first missing preference, never a skipped one — so answers in any order collect prefs without re-asking known ones. A mid-flow question goes to the same endpoint and gets a grounded model answer, then the conversation continues with the next question. Chips send their label as the user message (the model, not the chip, decides what was understood). Salary has its own amount + currency input; the amount is sent as a "`60000 GBP`" message and confirmed like any other extraction. Skip is local-only (never sent to the server). Progress is `wizard.chatPrefProgress` ("已了解 X / 8 项") with the still-missing items listed (`wizard.chatPrefRemaining` + `wizard.chatPrefName*` short labels) — never "第 n / 8 题". Done → summary bubble + finish button (unchanged). Pet face/moods, restart button and visual design kept. Before the first turn the pet asks the first missing preference with the existing `wizard.chatQ*` bubble + chips — no model call is spent on the hello. After that the model's lines carry the conversation. Locale change re-fetches model-led turns in the new locale; the re-fetch refreshes the WORDING only — its `extracted` is ignored, so a locale switch never applies anything. Offline fallback: any pref-chat failure (network, 404 for an endpoint not deployed yet, or `ai_status` "unavailable") switches to the guided queue (the pre-round-7 flow) with the honest `wizard.chatPrefOffline` line + a retry action (`wizard.chatPrefRetry`) that returns to the model-led chat and re-sends the failed message so the user's words are not dropped. `mapLocalAnswer` stays ONLY as the offline fallback's understander; `/profile/parse` confirmation flow kept for guided mode. |

| 153 | Pref-chat live failures: model latency vs timeouts + empty country retrieval (round 7 fix, 2026-11-26) | Root cause (diagnosed live 2026-11-26 ~17:13-17:16, before any code change): (1) Live DeepSeek calls via the shenjyai gateway took ~9-12 s each. The old budgets were 12 s first call / 8 s repair — the 8 s repair budget was SHORTER than one call, so any first-attempt validation failure fell back; the 12 s first-call budget was marginal (one live success took 11.9 s). The no-scope cold question systematically needed the repair: the model answers a recommendation question with an inference claim lacking the required explicit inference marker ("爱丁堡最适合你" — proven locally: candidate output fails with "inference claim without explicit inference language"), and/or states a wrong fee number (numeric cross-check). The CH-scoped case usually passed first try because the honest answer is a limitation/fact, not a recommendation. One 1.8 s failure was a fast transient ProviderError (gateway blip). The failure is intermittent, not systematic: the same CH+cool input later succeeded live (11.9 s, honest "no cool city in Switzerland"). (2) Case 1: `_detect_country` DID recognise 瑞士 → CH, but `_retrieve` only added country-scoped cities when a climate class was wanted, so a country-selection turn with no climate wish retrieved ZERO sources and the model claimed "no verifiable Swiss data". Fixes: `ai.provider.DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS` 20_000 → 60_000 (the spec Ch13/Ch17 20 s ceiling no longer matches observed gateway latency); first-call timeout 12 s → 30 s, repair 8 s → 20 s in both `ai.pref_chat.pref_answer` and `ai.chat.answer` (worst case 50 s, inside the frontend's ~90 s patient budget); `_retrieve` now always adds the scoped countries' own cities (`_country_cities` → `_climate_source`) even with no wanted class — pet path unaffected (it passes no countries); new shared `ai.chat.evidence_cities_line` (deterministic, 6 locales, at most 3 cities: city + actual class + verified count, parsed from the climate-source excerpts) replaces the generic "I don't know" first line in `pref_chat._fallback_result` and `chat._template_result` ONLY when climate sources were retrieved and the template's first line is the generic unknown line — any other template line is untouched, and with no evidence the generic unknown line stands; prompt schemas (`PREF_CHAT_SCHEMA`, `CHAT_SCHEMA`) now tell the model to phrase recommendations as kind "recommendation", never "inference", and that inference claims need explicit markers. Failure-class-only logging added to both chat paths (`logger.info` with kind/latency_ms/error count — never message, history, extracted data or key material) so a recurrence is visible in the Render log. Tests: `tests/test_pref_chat.py` +5 (country-turn retrieval, Switzerland sources non-empty, fallback-with-sources states cities, fallback-without-sources still says unknown, CH+cool sources asserted), `tests/test_ai_chat.py` +2 (evidence_cities_line unit, chat fallback states Edinburgh/Durham/Lund). Full suite 315 passed; build passes. |

The audit's own long /match requests plausibly left such requests running server-side. The #155 fix removes the multi-minute blockage class for /match. | 193 | R11-W1 F1 root cause: wizard remounted the whole page on every step | The step-3→4 `next()` handler itself cannot hang — it is synchronous (`canNext()` true unconditionally for step 3, then `setStep(s+1)`). The hang was in what the step change triggered: the wizard root div carried `key={step}`, so every step change unmounted and rebuilt the entire wizard, including the `usePrograms()` effect, which re-fired the patient `fetchPrograms()` (90 s retry budget) on every step transition; a click landing mid-remount hit a detached DOM node and was lost. Matches the audit exactly (two dead clicks, third landing after the fetch settled, ~1–2 min lost). Fix: `key={step}` removed from the root div (fetch fires once on mount), kept only on a wrapper around the step content so the entrance animation still replays; step changes run inside `useTransition` (Next/Back disable + spinner while committing, double-clicks can't skip a step). |
| 194 | R11-W1 F2: homepage "浏览全部课程" link — no code cause found | The link is a plain Next `<Link href="/programs">` — no ViewTransitionLink, no onClick, nothing on the click path can block navigation; /programs renders fast (SSR 0.013–0.05 s). The "homepage renders all 894 programmes" hypothesis is wrong: live `/api/programs` with no limit returns only 8 items, so the homepage renders 8 cards. No auto-advance timers move the link (FanCarousel has none; pet animations touch only the pet button). What remains is the live backend's slowness (18.2 s for that curl): while the homepage's patient `fetchPrograms` resolves, the programme section re-renders and briefly shifts the link's position — enough for a stability-waiting browser tool's clicks to time out, while a direct /programs URL works. Recorded as a browser-tool/environment artifact tied to slow backend fetch resolution; no code changed for F2. |
| 195 | R11-W1 F7: homepage backend status line | Homepage now uses the same shared `useBackendStatus()` hook and the exact `/programs` pattern (`<p role="status">` with checking/live/offline spans), placed under the programmes-section header. The old line that derived offline status from the page's own `usePrograms` fetch was removed (the same self-derived pattern round 9/R9 eliminated elsewhere) to avoid two contradictory status lines. |
| 196 | R11-W2 F3: fail-fast diagnosis — the 6–17 s is gateway latency, not our budgets | Measured, not assumed. Per-turn model-call count is exactly 1 (chat.py/pref_chat.py call `provider.chat` once, no repair retry; retrieval ~0.00 s). The backend already failed fast on a fast gateway error before this round (MockTransport 500 → endpoint template in 0.29 s/0.10 s; via the Next.js proxy 0.077 s/0.041 s). Live healthy call measured 7.21 s (ai_status available); down-gateway calls take 11–15 s to fail (parent curls: 11.1 s, 15.2 s, unavailable). The 6–17 s range is fully explained by model-call latency, which the 15 s budget cannot cut without clipping healthy calls — 15 s budget kept. The one real bug found: `httpx.Client()` with default `trust_env=True` parses environment proxy vars at construction; unparseable values (e.g. bracketed IPv6 in `NO_PROXY`, present in this sandbox) raise `httpx.InvalidURL` (a `ValueError`, not `HTTPError`), escaping the `ProviderError` contract and 500ing the whole chat endpoint instead of falling back to the template (reproduced live: `{"code":"INTERNAL_ERROR","message":"Invalid port: ':1]'"}`, no fallback). Fix: `trust_env=False` in `_client`/`_async_client` (no proxy var is part of the documented config surface; also keeps the key off any unexpected egress proxy) + `except ValueError → ProviderError(kind="bad_url")` in `chat()`/`achat()` as defence in depth. New tests assert a fast-500 gateway returns the template fallback in < 5 s. |
| 197 | R11-W2 F5: RecChat double fee label | `comment()` in RecChat.tsx built the fee text with `formatFeeItemWithLabel` ("学费：CHF 4,380 每年…") and then wrapped it in `wizard.recChatFee` ("学费 {fee}。") → "学费 学费：…". Fix: `feeItemWithApprox` gained a `withLabel` flag (default `true`, so the card fee row is unchanged); `comment()` now passes `false`, using label-free `formatFeeItem`. After: "学费 CHF 4,380 每年（按参考汇率约合 GBP 4,018 · 2026-11-26）。" — single label, fee sentence and approx-GBP intact. |
| 198 | R11-W3 F4: NTU fee reason — new `fee-band-unlabelled` key | The backend engine detail was already correct ("Non-subsidised fee band … is not labelled on the official fee table…"), but `adapter._derive_reason_key` mapped any detail containing "fee band" to `fee-residency-unknown`, rendering "学费取决于你的居留身份/学费津贴状态，未提供该信息。" — false for a profile where residency (overseas) was provided. Fix: `_derive_reason_key` returns new key `fee-band-unlabelled` when the detail contains "not labelled" (checked before the generic "fee band" branch; only the NTU detail contains that phrase — verified by grep). Frontend mirror (`api.ts`, NTU block only): `reasonKey` is `fee-band-unlabelled` when the resolved residency is `sg_intl_nogrant`, `fee-residency-unknown` only when residency itself is missing/unknown; NUS block untouched. Locale key `reason.fee-band-unlabelled` added in all 6 locales (zh-CN: "官方学费表未标注该课程属于实验类还是非实验类收费档，因此无法确定学费。"). NTU fee stays UNKNOWN by design; no band invented, S$40,600 lab figure not used as a threshold. Test `test_ntu_bcomp_fee_reason_key_band_unlabelled` asserts the audit profile maps to `fee-band-unlabelled` and a no-residency profile still maps to `fee-residency-unknown`. |
| 199 | R11-W3 F6: clear-data now clears the welcome banner | `WelcomeBanner` held its own local open/closed state and never heard about `clearData`, so it kept showing "欢迎回来！你的资格检查已完成" until manually closed. Fix: the store exposes a `bannerClearNonce` counter, bumped by `clearData`; `WelcomeBanner` closes itself immediately when the nonce changes. Deliberate: the `sessionStorage 'pp-welcome-shown'` flag is left set, so the banner stays gone for the rest of the session after clearing (a new session still shows it, per the existing every-session rule). |

| 225 | Round 13 P1: preference-summary dedupe regression — root cause was a compound note part, not the dedupe logic | The audit summary "凉爽 · 瑞士 · 环境优美；校园氛围 · 大学城·校园氛围 · …" showed "校园氛围" twice because the countries note is built by joining reasons with '；' (saveReason in PrefChat, profilePatchFromExtracted in chat.ts), so ONE part ("环境优美；校园氛围") held two values and neither whole string contains the other — the Round 10 whole-part containment check could not see it. Fix in the single summary builder (prefSummary, preferences.ts — used by results, report, PrefChat, pet): split every part into atoms on [；;、，] first (never '·' or ',', which live inside the campus label and the multi-country list), then the existing whole-atom containment dedupe (keep longer); a NOTE atom may additionally lose a '·'-segment that duplicates another surviving atom, the label itself is never rewritten. Audit profile now renders "凉爽 · 瑞士 · 环境优美 · 大学城·校园氛围 · 苏黎世 · 留在留学国家 · 60000 GBP · 适中 · 小班研讨" with 校园氛围 exactly once. | 232 | Round 13 W1: backend fail-fast — batched /whynot template-only, /simulate explain and /improvement-plan off the event loop with a hard 15 s model cap, per-user AI rate-limit bucket via X-Forwarded-For | Root cause (measured, diagnosis confirmed in code): the results page fires ONE batched POST /whynot with every NOT_MET programme (80 ids in the audit profile) and the old handler looped per programme through whynot_with_model -> explain_structured_with_status -> one SYNCHRONOUS provider.chat (30 s first + 20 s repair) inline in the async handler — 3 ids = 33.2 s, /health timed out at 30 s mid-run, 80 ids ≈ 880 s of total backend unresponsiveness. That single wedge explains the simulate, plan AND chat browser failures (every other request queues behind the blocked single uvicorn worker while the frontend's 25 s attempts abort and retry to the 90 s budget). Fixes: (1) /whynot is template-only — no provider is constructed in the batch, model_note always null, ai_status always "unavailable"; the deterministic template already carries the verified why-not (same fix /match got in Round 8). whynot_with_model is KEPT as the single-programme on-demand helper (still unit-tested); if ever wired to an endpoint it must run in a worker thread with a <=15 s budget, never in a per-programme loop. (2) /simulate explain=true runs explain_simulation via asyncio.to_thread + asyncio.wait_for(15 s); _model_explain_simulation is a single 15 s attempt with NO repair retry (a repair can only return after the caller gave up), and explain_simulation catches any model-path exception so it never raises/500s — template fallback always. (3) /improvement-plan runs create_plan via to_thread + wait_for(15 s) with a template-plan rebuild on timeout; _model_tasks is a single 15 s attempt (was 12 s + 12 s repair = ~24 s, exactly on the 25 s abort boundary), provider failure -> template tasks, validation failure -> template tasks (keeps the "did not pass validation" wording). (4) Rate limiting: new _client_ip() trusts the first X-Forwarded-For entry (Vercel sends it) and falls back to request.client.host; the middleware and the /simulate manual AI-bucket check both use it, so all browser users no longer share one 10/min AI bucket keyed on the Vercel egress IP. Chat endpoints untouched (already asyncio.to_thread, 15 s, no repair). Uvicorn worker count unchanged (considered and deferred — the root fixes remove the blockage; more workers would only mask it and cost free-tier memory). Measurement note: httpx MockTransport does NOT enforce timeouts (verified: a 20 s mock sleep runs the full 20 s despite timeout=5, sync and async), so the 15 s cap lives at the async boundary (wait_for) as well as in provider.chat(timeout_s=15); through TestClient the response is only delivered once the orphan worker thread finishes, so the endpoint slow-gateway test bounds at 25 s (old code took ~40 s there) while the direct-coroutine cap test and the real-uvicorn runs pin 15 s. | 242 | Round 16 W1: WHY order fixed at the source (question line withheld when WHY present) + WHY as first-class turn state + input/send/skip live during confirm | Root cause (WHY order): the first reply's `lines` ENDED with the next-question line (`backend/ai/pref_chat.py::_render_pref_lines` and `_fallback_result` append `_question_line(next_question)` last) while the WHY rendered as a separate bubble after the whole reply, so the visible order was user → record + climate question → WHY → confirm. Fix: when a turn carries a WHY follow-up, the backend WITHHOLDS the question line from `lines` — the WHY IS the turn's question — and `next_question` still names the deferred preference; the frontend renders the deferred question inside the turn only after the WHY is answered or skipped (reuses existing `wizard.chatQ*` keys; frontend strings differ from backend `_NEXT_QUESTION_LINES`, so no string-matching is used). Bubble order is now a pure function `prefTurnBubbles` in `frontend/src/lib/chat.ts` (user → reply → WHY question → WHY answer → deferred question); locale re-fetch strip drops the trailing question line so it never appears twice. Root cause (WHY detached): the WHY lived only in transient `awaitingReason`; `saveReason` appended the answer to `confirmedNotes` (bottom bubbles) and clearing `awaitingReason` erased the question. Fix: `PrefTurnWhy` (`{countries, note?, answer, skipped}`) on `PrefTurn.why`, set when armed (incl. gateway-down preserve path, anchored with the turn id), resolved by `answerTurnWhy`/`skipTurnWhy`, persisted in the pp-prefchat blob (old blobs parse; malformed `why` dropped, turn kept); skip-then-confirm no longer re-arms a resolved WHY. Root cause (input disabled): input/send/skip were `disabled={thinking || (!!pendingExtract && !awaitingReason)}` — dead the moment the WHY was answered or on resume with a pending extraction. Fix: `disabled={thinking}` only (model + guided); `submitModelText` no longer returns early on `pendingExtract`; a send during a pending confirm folds the pending extraction into the request's `known` (`mergeKnownWithExtracted`) and a new reply's extraction folds into the pending confirm (`mergeExtracted`) instead of overwriting it (overwriting would have silently dropped Switzerland). | 243 | Round 16 W2: devil arithmetic — model-invented difference ('相差1项' for 14 vs 11) now enforced against engine-computed differences; citation checker blind spot documented and partially closed | Root cause: MODEL-written, not template — `grep 相差` across backend/frontend/tests returns zero hits; the deterministic template line only lists each city with its own count ('苏黎世（四季分明，14 个项目……）、洛桑（四季分明，11 个项目……）'), reproduced with no '相差'. The model received both counts twice (English conflict detail + climate evidence excerpts) and subtracted them itself, getting 14−11=1; the devil prompt's rule ('state only numbers that appear in that cited evidence') did not forbid it ('1' arguably appears as a substring of '14'/'11'). Why the checker missed it (two independent reasons, backend/ai/grounding.py): (1) `_numbers_in()` deliberately skips small bare integers, so the '1' in '相差1项' is never extracted (reproduced: `_numbers_in(...)` == `[]`); (2) even if extracted, the check is substring membership — '1' would PASS as a substring of '14'/'11'. The checker validates token presence, never arithmetic — it fundamentally cannot validate derived numbers on its own. Fix at the generation site + checker enforcement: climate conflicts now carry engine-computed `count_difference` (|top − second| city count; audit: 3); the model envelope carries it as a field plus 'Verified difference between the two largest city counts: N programmes.' in the detail; new `_devil_allowed_differences()` (all pairwise diffs of listed city counts; audit {3}, DE {11,16,5}); new `stated_differences()` + `difference_errors()` in grounding.py; `check_model_output(..., allowed_differences=None)` checks every claim kind (not just facts) plus the summary/action explanations; devil prompt now says to use ONLY the supplied `count_difference`, never subtract in its head (advisory; the checker enforces). Tests: tests/test_r16_w2_devil_difference.py (12 new) — pre-fix the wrong payload validated with zero errors; post-fix it is rejected ('claims[0] text states a difference of 1, which is not an engine-computed difference (allowed: 3)') and a mock model stating it falls back to the template; correct '相差3项' validates clean and reaches the user as AI; DE case (Munich 17 / Berlin 6 / Heidelberg 1) → allowed {11,16,5}; English '1 more' rejected, '3 more' passes. Residuals (stated plainly): paths passing `allowed_differences=None` (explain.py, normal chat) still have NO difference checking; the counts themselves (14, 11) are still not cross-checked (`_numbers_in` skips small bare integers by design); with 3 listed cities a wrong difference coinciding with another pair's real difference passes (bounded). |
| 244 | Round 16 W3: simulate fast-skip on a flapping gateway — probe now confirms on the real model path; /simulate handler skips the model attempt on a cached 'unavailable' verdict | Root cause (measured, not guessed): the probe only checked `GET /models` — the flapping gateway answers the list endpoint (200) while `POST /chat/completions` hangs, so the probe said 'available' in 0.07 s and the skip never fired; the 15 s explain cap (`asyncio.wait_for(15.0s)` + chat `timeout_s=15.0s`) was the mechanism, not the cause (15.5 s model + ~0.5 s engine + Render overhead = the observed ~18–19 s). The panel DID consult `modelAvailability()` via `simulateMulti`, but the backend did not consult the cached probe verdict — it relied solely on the breaker, which only helps AFTER one slow failure. Fix: (1) `backend/ai/provider.py` — `probe_live()` now confirms on the real model path: after `GET /models` 200 it runs one 1-token chat call (`_probe_chat_call()`, `_PROBE_CHAT_TIMEOUT_S = 7.0 s`) before reporting live; a hang returns False and trips the breaker, a fast failure returns False without tripping. 7 s (not 5 s — tried first, rejected) because the real gateway's healthy tiny-chat was measured at ~3.4 s on 2026-11-27 ~03:36, so 7 s is ~2x headroom; 5 s would have turned ordinary slowness into false 'unavailable'. (2) `backend/app/main.py` — new `_model_skip_cached()`: the /simulate handler skips the model attempt (before the AI-bucket check, so template-only calls consume no 10/min quota) when the breaker is open OR a fresh cached probe said 'unavailable'; non-blocking lock read; cold cache means attempt as before. Measured on a live harness (real uvicorn + mock gateway, 373 ids): flap probe → simulate 15.47 s → probe 7.15 s 'unavailable', simulate 0.50 s template (click path 7.65 s; 0.50 s with a fresh cache); hang-all 2nd call 0.47 s; fail-500 probe 0.10 s 'unavailable', simulate 0.46 s with NO extra gateway POST; slow-chat (2 s) probe still 'available' (no false negative); healthy simulate 0.83 s model; slow-healthy (10 s chat) 10.53 s model. Residual worst case (stated plainly): with NO prior probe and a closed breaker, the FIRST simulate on a hanging gateway still burns the full 15 s before the template (measured 15.5 s); the breaker then protects every later call for 60 s. Tests: tests/test_r16_w3_simulate_failfast.py (10 new; real in-process HTTP gateway — httpx MockTransport does not enforce timeouts); one Round 14 test updated to the new probe contract (1 GET + 1 chat POST, still cached). |

| 246 | Round 17 W1: country preference can never be silently lost — WHY answer is implicit confirmation + confirm survives a drop; WHY answer renders as a user bubble | Root cause (loss, verified with evidence, not assumed): turn-1 extraction {countries:[CH], note:"那边环境优美"} lived only in unconfirmed frontend pendingExtract; answering the WHY called saveReason, which stored ONLY the note and never touched pendingExtract; the drop ran preserveFailedAnswer → enterGuidedMode, which did setPendingExtract(null) and discarded CH; and a note alone counts as the countries preference answered (isPrefKnown), so the guided queue skipped the countries question and the note masqueraded as the country. Measured before-state: summary "凉爽 · 喜欢湖光山色·空气清新·安静宜居" (no 瑞士), top 2 recommendations alberta-cs-ai-bsc-2027, anu-computing-bachelor-2027 — exactly the tester finding. Fix, two halves: (1) WHY answer = implicit confirmation — new pure helper splitWhyConfirmedCountries in lib/chat.ts, used by saveReason: answering the WHY lands that country's extraction in the Profile in the same update as the note; only the WHY's countries land, the rest stays pending for the confirm bubble. (2) Confirm survives a drop — enterGuidedMode and retryModelChat no longer discard the pending extraction, and the guided branch re-offers the same confirm bubble as the model branch; only confirm / dismiss / restart clear it. Deliberately NOT changed: isPrefKnown/countKnownPrefs/firstMissingPref still treat a note alone as the countries preference answered (designed behaviour for an unlisted country named in words); after fixes 1+2 the note can no longer masquerade as the country in the WHY flow. Backend unchanged — the loss was entirely frontend-side; new backend tests pin the post-fix contract (known={countries:[CH], note} → climate turn does not re-fire the WHY, model-up and offline). Root cause (WHY rendering): prefTurnBubbles already returned a separate whyAnswer bubble (R16), but renderPrefTurns rendered it inside a white assistant bubble. Fix: whyAnswer now renders as a cyan right-aligned user bubble, acknowledgement (wizard.chatOpThanks) a separate white assistant line after it. Rendered order: user cyan → assistant white (reply) → assistant white (WHY question) → user cyan (WHY answer) → assistant white (ack) → assistant white (deferred climate question). P1 order nit judged ACCEPTABLE, not fixed: the WHY is the turn's only question and the climate question line is withheld; the facts in bubbles ①② are verified evidence lines (in the template path the first line IS the evidence line) — stripping them would gut the reply's evidence. P2 dedupe residual: the second copy is the pet's collapsed title bar (pet.prefSaved, CompassPet.tsx:1280), outside the worker's file scope. Tests: tests/test_r17_w1.py (5 new; node harness exact tester path + source pins); pref-chat subset 71 passed. Measured after-state (exact tester path): summary "凉爽 · 瑞士 · 喜欢湖光山色·空气清新·安静宜居", top 2 epfl-cs-bsc-2027, eth-cs-bsc-2027 (both CH). Residuals: turn-less WHY answers (guided queue, old blobs) still render via confirmedNotes as a white "noted" bubble (persistence-shape change would break R15/R16 pinned contracts); a bare reason typed as a first countries answer in the guided queue with no country named anywhere still stores as note-only by design. |
| 247 | Round 17 W2: offline devil template now states the engine-computed difference | Decision: YES, state it. The template was never numbers-light — it already lists the verified counts (Zurich 14, Lausanne 11), fees and other engine numbers — so stating the comparison those counts invite fits its role; leaving it unstated was the gap re-test task 11 found (14 and 11 listed, no difference at all, model offline the whole run). Fix: new _DEVIL_DIFFERENCE table (6 locales) + _devil_difference_sentence() in backend/ai/chat.py, appended to the climate line in devil_conflict_line (same line, ≤3-line cap unaffected); en "Zurich has 3 more verified programmes than Lausanne.", zh-CN "苏黎世与洛桑相差3项。" (es/fr/pt-BR/ar localised equivalents, existing city-name table, canonical name when unlisted — no invented translations). The sentence uses ONLY the engine-computed count_difference on the conflict; the template never subtracts — a regression test pins this with a hand-built field of 7 over cities 14/11 (template states 7, proving it renders the field verbatim). Only the top-two difference is stated; other pairwise differences (Germany: 16, 5) stay model-only and remain allowed by the checker. Omitted when no usable difference (missing key, single city, 0 tie). The model envelope no longer appends a second "Verified difference…" sentence (devil_conflict_line now carries it); count_difference field stays in the envelope; prompt instruction (use ONLY that number) unchanged. en/zh wordings match stated_differences() patterns, so the template's own stated number is checkable: stated_differences(template) == [3] (audit), [11] (Germany). Three R16 assertions had to change because they pinned the old behaviour by design (template-never-states-a-difference renamed to assert 相差3项; wrong-difference fallback now asserts the fallback contains 相差3项, never 相差1项; envelope wording "3 programmes" → "3 more verified programmes"). Tests: tests/test_r17_w2_devil_template.py (10 new); relevant subset 54 passed; R16 checker coverage intact. Residual: stated_differences() has no patterns for es/fr/pt-BR/ar, so a wrong difference stated by the MODEL in those locales would still slip past the checker (pre-existing gap; the template itself is engine-sourced in all locales, so it cannot state a wrong one). |

| 249 | Round 18 W1: step-8 direct re-entry false empty state (hydration race) + leaked {total}/{country} placeholders + clear-data step reset | Root cause (verified with the real compiled libs + audit profile): React runs child effects before the provider hydration effect — on a fresh load of /wizard with stored step 7, the step-7 effect ran while profile was still emptyProfile (track ''), the candidate filter matched nothing → recs=[] (and targetProgramIds=[]), and with deps [step] only the effect never re-ran after hydration; RecChat then saw recs=[] with prefCountries=['CH'] and rendered the no-country notice + empty state. | 250 | Round 18 W2: devil/simulate latency — breaker 60→180 s, ai_status short-circuit on open breaker, probe warming every 45 s, auto-suggest gated on known-down verdict | Root cause (measured on a real-socket mock gateway, audit scale 894 programmes / 601 matchable): a single devil turn cannot take 25–30 s through onSend (fetchJsonChat 18 s attempt; backend burns exactly one 15 s model budget when the breaker is closed, then renders the template) — the browser's 25–30 s was the auto-suggest turn on panel open (greet() → askSuggest, ~10 s while the gateway was briefly up; a success CLEARS the breaker) PLUS the devil turn's 15 s burn as the first call after the gateway died, experienced as one stretch of "正在查已核实资料…". Systematic defect: the breaker only tripped AFTER a slow failure and lasted 60 s — the same 60 s as the probe cache — so both expired together and the next action after a minute of quiet paid the full 15 s again (parent's 10:18 check was fast on warm caches; the 10:23 clicks were not). Simulate 10–30 s: same expiry — cold click paid probe 7.23 s (flap) + engine 2.42 s = 9.65 s vs 2.88 s warm; /ai/status RE-PROBED even with the breaker open (stale-cache read cost 7.14 s although the breaker already knew). Fixes: (1) GATEWAY_BREAKER_COOLDOWN_S 60→180 s — the breaker now OUTLIVES the 60 s probe cache; failed probes re-trip it, the probe bypasses it and clears it on success. (2) ai_status: while the breaker is open, a stale cache answers "unavailable" at once (stale cached "unavailable" returned as-is; no cached verdict → synthetic unavailable, probed: False); a stale cached "available" never short-circuits. (3) warmModelAvailability() + MODEL_AVAILABILITY_WARM_MS 45_000 in lib/api.ts; SimulatePanel warms on mount, every 45 s, and on window focus (SimulatePanel mounts on the results page unconditionally, so both caches stay fresh and the breaker stays tripped while down). (4) At integration, W2's handed E1 applied: askSuggest gates on modelAvailability() === 'unavailable' → goOffline() at once (the offline catalogue is exactly what a template reply produces there; ~ms when the verdict is cached). E2 (warm before a user-sent turn) deliberately SKIPPED at integration: it would add ~3.5 s to a healthy-path devil send when the cache is stale, and warming + 180 s breaker + E1 already cover the common paths. Measured after: /ai/status stale-cache + breaker open 7.14 s → 0.00 s; simulate click warm 2.88 s → 0.48 s; devil breaker-open 0.01 s. Residual (pinned in tests): the FIRST model call after the gateway dies (cold breaker, cold probe cache) still burns the 15 s budget — no cache can know the gateway died since the last probe. Tests: tests/test_r18_w2_latency.py (10 new; real-socket mock gateway). |
| 251 | Round 18 W3: contradictory devil labels fixed; pet preference repetition fixed; climate wording softened (judged); English residuals in programme-detail minimums traced | (1) Labels: the devil bubble rendered two labels from two independent paths — the boundary line used t('devil.boundary') unconditionally (every locale claims AI generation) while renderTurn's 'ask' case prepended pet.offline to an offline template turn. No backend change. (2) Repetition: the results page's own summary + pet title-bar strip pet.prefSaved (CompassPet.tsx:1280, every page) + prefNote opening bubble pet.greetingKnown (pushed by greet(), panel auto-reopens on /results) = 3 occurrences. Fix inside CompassPet only: the strip is suppressed on /results (hasPreferences(profile) && pathname !== '/results'; the results page has its own results.prefEdit link), and on /results the opening bubble uses new key pet.greetingKnownShort (all 6 locales, no {prefs} placeholder). Off /results both stay as before; the summary now shows exactly once on the results page. (3) Climate: JUDGED fix the wording — the devil's facts are right and flagging the clash is its job, but the old line overstated it: the engine (backend/domain/climate.py) rates cool-vs-four_seasons as "neutral" and climate "never produces NOT_MET… it only orders results", while the devil's detector treats any class mismatch as a conflict and the template stated "其中没有凉爽气候的城市" in absolute terms. Fix (wording only): one clause appended to the climate template in _DEVIL_T, all 6 locales — zh-CN "气候只是偏好，不影响资格，只影响排序。" Climate data untouched; no cool Swiss city invented. (4) English residuals (ETH CS/CSE BSc 2027): "Main teaching language…" is an official-source excerpt (language_of_instruction.note in expansion.json, ETH source URL), rendered with lang="en" and localised surrounding labels — correct as-is, NOT translated. "3 years (180 credits)" is a data duration field formatDuration can't parse — fixed with per-programme keys program.duration.eth-cs-bsc-2027 / eth-cse-bsc-2027 in all 6 locales (established pattern), no data file changed. "No IELTS component minimum published" is UI-generated copy — NOT fixed: hardcoded English fallback in frontend/src/lib/api.ts:466 (expComponentExpected) mirrored in backend/app/adapter.py:591,648 — localising it means threading locale through the expansion evaluator in both mirrors (neither call site has locale in scope); recorded as a residual for a future round (a localised equivalent already exists, e.g. zh-CN rule.nus-language-components.expected "未公布雅思单项最低要求"). Tests: tests/test_r18_w3_labels.py (14 new). Locale keys 959 → 963 × 6 (4 new keys: devil.offlineBoundary, pet.greetingKnownShort, program.duration.eth-cs-bsc-2027, program.duration.eth-cse-bsc-2027). |

| 253 | Round 19 W1: devil reply sentence order (difference must be last) + exactly one disclosure line on an offline devil bubble | Nit 1: `_devil_template_result` appended `_t(locale, "next")` after the conflict lines, so the difference sentence sat mid-reply. The model path had the same bug (`_render_lines` appends "next" when len < 3) — fixed the same way via `stated_differences()`, en/zh only (es/fr/pt-BR/ar have no checker patterns yet — pre-existing Round 17 gap, not widened). Multi-conflict replies keep "next" (difference is mid-reply by construction; reordering conflicts out of scope). Nit 2: an offline devil turn rendered TWO disclosure strings in its bubble — boundary `devil.offlineBoundary` (CompassPet.tsx:1321) + body `pet.offline` prepended in `renderTurn` 'ask' case (~line 999). Fix: `turn.offline && !turn.devil` for the body line. Citation correction: the task cited CompassPet.tsx:1425, but that is the PANEL FOOTER status span (connectivity chrome + retry button, no turn concept) — deliberately untouched; the per-turn body line is the ~999 prepend. Non-devil offline turns keep the body line; live devil turns keep `devil.boundary`. |
| 254 | Round 19 W2: simulate panel header claimed "AI 生成的解读" with the model down + first-click timing measured | Nit 3: `sim.boundary` ("AI 生成的解读…") rendered unconditionally at both SimulatePanel boundary sites even when the summary said "规则摘要——未连接 AI 模型". Fix: new key `sim.boundaryTemplate` × 6 (template wording, second half verbatim from the existing boundary); both sites share `explanationIsModel = result?.explanation?.source === 'model'` and render `sim.boundary` only for a real model run — pre-run header shows the template wording (no AI reading exists yet; hiding it was rejected — the boundary line is a standing constraint). First-click timing (measured on a real-socket mock flapping gateway, 601 matchable ids, 3 scenarios, explain:true): cold first click via the frontend gate = probe 7.15 s + engine 0.47 s = 7.61 s; warm second click 0.49 s; cold raw explain:true with no gate (verdict 'unknown') 15.48 s (full 15 s model budget, then breaker open). Warming verified correctly wired on mount (45 s interval + focus listener); it only helps if the results page has been open longer than the mount probe (~7 s). No cheap/safe fix exists — behaviour left alone. Task 13's "≤60 s" was a minute-level clock, not a real cost. |
| 255 | Round 19 W3: mixed-language duration on the step-8 UZH card fixed; nit 5 (Switzerland + cool repeats country + climate once) judged, not fixed | Nit 4 root cause: `zurich-informatics-bsc-2027` had NO `program.duration.<id>` key in any locale (ETH siblings did, Round 18), and the generic fallback regex in `formatDuration` could not match a parenthetical, so data duration "3 years (180 ECTS)" fell through as raw English. Fix, two layers: (1) per-programme key × 6; (2) generic fallback now parses "(N credits)" / "(N ECTS)" / "(N semesters, N ECTS)" via new `format.credit(s)` / `format.ects` / `format.semester(s)` keys, locale threaded through as an optional 3rd parameter (all call sites + `formatProgramLine`). ECTS stays "ECTS" in every locale (standard acronym, like IELTS); unparseable shapes still fall back to the raw verified value. Measured: UZH zh-CN "3 years (180 ECTS)" → "3 年（180 ECTS）"; ETH MSc "2 years (4 semesters, 120 ECTS)" → "2 年（4 学期，120 ECTS）". Nit 5 root cause, two halves: (1) backend `_deterministic_extract` extracts countries + note only, never enums — a "凉爽" turn with known {CH} extracts {} and the template reply re-asks climate (the backend never re-asks a *known* pref; climate was never answered in its view); (2) frontend `mapFailedPrefAnswer` DOES understand "凉爽" → cool and applies it, but the guided queue re-renders the answered country + climate blocks as history, so both questions display a second time. No one-line fix exists (extending the offline extractor to enums is a small feature with a question-guard it lacks; hiding the guided-history blocks would hide the conflict opinion itself). Acceptable: nothing is lost, no re-answer is forced, the conflict is stated honestly; pinned by a lock-in test (`test_nit5_offline_climate_answer_not_extracted_by_backend`) that must be updated deliberately if the extractor is ever extended. |

| 257 | Round 20 W1: pet panel footer claimed "AI 回答" on a stale session verdict | Root cause: the footer ternary itself was already correct (`pet.aiLive` shows only when `aiLive === true`) — the leak was staleness. Footer ternary and the per-turn body line (`turn.offline && !turn.devil`) untouched; first-open language unchanged. Verification: node harness /tmp/r20-w1-harness.js 23 passed (source audit + state-machine simulation of the task-14 scenario: old trace reproduces the bug, new trace shows checking → offline, never the claim while down; claim appears only after a fresh confirmed available verdict in the up case; all 6 locales carry all 6 footer keys); tsc clean; check-locales passed (970 keys × 6, no new keys); build pass (12/12). Residuals: a probe can confirm `available` and the gateway can still flap down before the next turn (probe-then-chat window, inherent); a long-open panel's claim reflects the last confirmed verdict until the next turn or retry (timer re-probe considered and rejected as out of scope). |

| 260 | Round 21 W1: footer first-open false "AI 回答" — real source was the backend probe cache, not frontend persistence | Root cause (diagnosed with evidence, see /tmp/r21-w1-decisions.md): (1) backend /ai/status 60 s server-side cache, per-process, shared by ALL users/tabs (backend/app/main.py, _AI_STATUS_TTL_S = 60) — the parent's 13:21 curl ran a live probe that succeeded and cached available; the tester's 13:22 pet probe was served that cached available in ~0 s without touching the gateway, which had already flapped (gateway flaps on a minutes timescale; the probe is only a 1-token chat, weaker than a real turn — parent's numbers: /ai/status available 13:21, pref-chat flapping after 15.61 s). (2) The cached payload MISLABELS itself as fresh: ai_status() returned dict(cached) with "probed": True from probe time, so no caller could tell fresh from stale; the pet probe did not even read probed. (3) The checking line is invisible in practice: the warm-cache probe resolves in a fraction of a second, so the checking state flashes unseen (tester never saw it all run); plus a first-paint race — effects run after paint, so on re-open in the same SPA session (CompassPet never remounts) the panel paints once with the previous open's aiLive===true before the reset effect runs. aiLive itself is plain useState(null) — no localStorage/sessionStorage/module persistence holds it. Fix: cached serves now return {**cached, "probed": False, "cache_age_s": <age>}; new ?fresh=1 bypasses the 60 s cache read and probes live now (breaker-open fast path kept: breaker open still answers unavailable at once, no probe); fresh probe results refresh the cache with probed: True, cache_age_s: 0.0. New frontend/src/lib/aiStatus.ts (pure, zero-dep): freshAiVerdict() returns true only for ai_status==='available' AND probed===true, false for unavailable, null for available-but-unprobed (never accepted as live); AI_CHECKING_MIN_MS = 1200 + checkingDelayMs(); CompassPet probeAiStatus() probes /api/ai/status?fresh=1 and applies verdicts only via freshAiVerdict(); unconfirmed verdicts keep checking and re-probe fresh (max 2), then fall back to offline; open/devil probes hold checking >=1200 ms (seq-guarded timer, cleared on close); toggle(true) resets aiLive/offline/waking BEFORE setOpen(true), so the first paint of a re-opened panel can never show the previous open's claim. Footer JSX, retry condition, per-turn body line, first-open English default unchanged. Verification: tests/test_r21_w1_footer_probe.py (new, real mock gateway, replays task-15: prime available → gateway flaps → plain read still cached-available → ?fresh=1 must say unavailable): 5 failed before → 5 passed after (fresh flapping probe ~7 s); node harness /tmp/r21-w1-harness.mjs 20/20 after (transpiles the real aiStatus.ts, replays the task-15 timeline); tsc clean; adjacent suites test_r14_devil_and_failfast.py + test_r16_w3_simulate_failfast.py 25 passed, test_r18_w2_latency.py 10 passed. Residual: spamming open/close could hit the 60/min general rate-limit bucket; a 429 fails the probe to offline (safe direction, retry available). |
| 261 | Round 21 W2: /results programme cards show no duration at all | Root cause: the duration value was never missing — it was never rendered. The /results card JSX (frontend/src/app/results/page.tsx, the shown.map article) renders institution, name, hints, status chips, blockers, per-rule RuleRows, WhyNot and buttons, but never called formatDuration or formatProgramLine; duration is not a rule, so it could never appear via RuleRows — hence zero hits for "180 ECTS" on the page.
