| 262 | Round 21 W3: WHY helpfulness inconsistency — programme-scoping gap, not a data gap | Root cause (reproduced locally, no key, zh-CN): the same question — "What is the fee and IELTS requirement for this programme, and is it a good fit for me?" — got "ETH Zurich — BSc CS：学费CHF 4,380 / 年。" on the detail page but the exact tester wording "这个我从已核实的数据里查不到，不知道。" in the step-8 chat. The verified fee facts existed all along (all 5 Swiss UG computing programmes have verified CHF fees). The detail page scopes its question with the explicit "Programme id <id>" marker, so _retrieve brings that programme's verified sources and _template_result states its fee. RecChat sent only {message, locale, history} — no programme was ever in scope, so a deictic "this programme" / "这个专业" matched nothing and the verified fee facts were never queried. Secondary finding: even naming ETH does not fix it — the alias match returns both UG siblings (CS BSc + CSE BSc) and answers "ambiguous"; only the exact marker resolved it. Fix — caller-supplied programme scope (program_ids), end to end: backend/ai/chat.py answer(..., program_ids=None) with _clean_program_ids (verified catalogue only, deduped, max 10, unknown ids dropped); _retrieve adds the scoped programmes' enriched sources (+ ranking sources) to the evidence catalogue, exactly as the Programme id intent does (scope ids stay out of matched pids, so suggestion/ranking-overview gating is unchanged); _template_result gains a scope branch via _scope_line — fact question (fee/IELTS/deadline) + one in-scope programme → that programme's verified fact line in the template's own wording; several in scope → a which-one line naming them (6 locales); named-ETH ambiguity + one sibling in view → narrows to its fee; why-not question about an in-scope programme → a specific refusal naming the programme and the missing piece (the rule-by-rule evaluation is not in the chat). Guards: scope never overrides a template-answered programme and never fires on rank/climate/suggestion/country questions. backend/app/router_chat.py: ChatRequest.program_ids (max 10, verified ids only), passed through to chat_answer. frontend/src/components/RecChat.tsx: askModel and the locale re-fetch now send program_ids = the visible batch's ids (max 3 cards). No data invented; only verified engine facts reused. Verification: before (no scope) "这个我从已核实的数据里查不到，不知道。" → after (program_ids=["eth-cs-bsc-2027"]) "ETH Zurich — Bachelor of Science in Computer Science：学费CHF 4,380 / 年。" (+ official-page evidence_url); batch scope of 3 → which-one line naming all three; why-not + scope → specific refusal naming ETH CS BSc and the missing evaluation (genuinely no verified answer in the chat path). Tests: tests/test_r21_w3_why_scope.py (new) 13 passed; targeted regression files 138 passed; full suite 657 passed, 1 failed on test_openapi_matches_generated_copy (endpoint docstring now mentions program_ids) — fixed by regenerating docs/openapi.json with tools/generate_openapi.py, re-run 14 passed. Residual: the why-not refusal's evidence_url points to the programme's official page, not the detail-page WHY section — a UX call for a future round. |

| 264 | Round 22 W1: footer "AI 回答" leak DURING a question (task-16 residual) — first open PASSed, but asking a question inside the pet briefly showed "AI 回答 · 只用已核实资料" mid-lookup, then offline after the answer, model down the whole run | Root cause (diagnosed with evidence, see /tmp/r22-w1-decisions.md): the open probe is SLOW while a turn verdict is FAST. probe_live() is a 1-token chat (GET /models ≤3 s + max_tokens=1 confirmation ≤7 s), so the open probe can resolve ~7–10 s after open; meanwhile askSuggest's modelAvailability() fast path calls goOffline() within ~1–2 s of open — that is the offline line the tester saw first, while the open probe was still in flight. The gateway flaps, so the slow probe's 1-token call can succeed ("available", probed:true) while every real turn fails — new backend pin test proves at one moment the fresh probe says "available" AND the real turn answers template-only with ai_status "unavailable" (probe = max_tokens=1 call; turn = max_tokens=1200 call hanging past its 15 s budget). probeAiStatus's only staleness guard was probeSeqRef, which protects against newer PROBES, never against TURN verdicts — so the probe resolving "available" mid-lookup set aiLive=true, and the turn's template reply then set it back offline. Ruled out: modelAvailability() never sets aiLive; the locale re-fetch setAiLive(true) needs a locale switch (absent in task 16) and is real-turn evidence anyway; onSend/askSuggest set aiLive=true only after their own reply lands. Fix (frontend-only, no backend change): new pure probeVerdictApplies() in lib/aiStatus.ts — a probe verdict applies only when no turn is in flight AND no turn verdict has landed since the probe started; probeAiStatus captures the turn-verdict generation at probe start and drops stale verdicts in both applyVerdict and applyDown (still clears waking); turnInFlightRef set/cleared directly in onSend/askSuggest (finally + early return); turnVerdictGenRef bumped at all 8 turn-verdict sites (goOffline ×2, toOffline, locale model/template branches, onSend/askSuggest model/template branches); probe verdicts never bump the generation; locale re-fetch verdicts deliberately NOT gated (a real model reply is the strongest evidence). Preserved: first-open checking ≥1200 ms, offline line, devil reset/re-probe, retry path, unconfirmed-verdict re-probe. |
| 265 | Round 22 W2: WHY disambiguation still fails for a normal user (task-16 finding (c)) — Chinese display name "ETH Zurich 计算机科学理学学士学费多少？" mis-matched to MSc programmes (MSc in Computer Science, MSc in Data Science, MSc in Cyber Security…) and asked undergrad-or-masters; only the exact English full name got the fee line (CHF 4,380) | Root cause (reproduced offline, no-key provider, scope = 3 Swiss UG programmes): two compounding causes. (1) The matcher knew only English tokens and institution aliases — "计算机科学理学学士" carried no level word it recognised ("学士" was not in _UG_WORDS), so level was None and the "eth" alias branch returned every ETH programme, MSc first; the scoped programmes' verified name_localized titles were never consulted. (2) The matcher never received the track at all — RecChat never sent it, the endpoint had no track field, and answer()/_retrieve()/_scope_line() had no track parameter. Fix: (i) new _match_by_display_title in backend/ai/assistant.py matches a programme's verified degree-name strings — official English name plus every name_localized value — against the message, pool = visible batch when in scope else the whole catalogue; a degree name shared by several programmes narrows by an institution token ("ETH Zurich" / "eth" / "苏黎世联邦理工" / rankings name_zh); no token → stays ambiguous; no new data, no guessed translations. (ii) "学士" added to _UG_WORDS (parallel to "硕士" in _TM_WORDS; checked: no MSc Chinese name contains "学士"). (iii) Track threaded end to end: RecChat sends track: profile.track (both chat POSTs) → ChatRequest.track (invalid values dropped, never a 422) → answer() → _retrieve() → assistant.ask() / _match_programmes() / _scope_line(); every matcher branch's result is filtered by track; an explicitly named programme in the other track filters to [] (honest unknown), never a cross-track guess. (iv) Step-1 deictic "这个专业" stays the which-one line (genuinely ambiguous; R21 test pins it) but is now one-tap on the frontend: when the latest turn's reply contains "哪一门" / "Which one do you mean", RecChat renders chips naming the visible programmes; tapping one re-asks with the explicit "Programme id <id>." marker. Answering the top programme instead would have been a guess about which card the user meant. (v) _scope_line knock-on fixed: ambiguous now also covers the template's ambiguous-programme line, so a scope that narrows to one still answers — still guarded so rank+fee questions keep their rank answer. Note: the tester's live step 3 reportedly got the fee line while the offline repro got the two-sibling ambiguous line — unexplained live-run discrepancy (possibly a different batch/scope), now moot: the offline path answers step 3 deterministically. |

| 268 | Round 23 W1: chip tap leaks the internal programme id into the user bubble (re-test task 17) | Root cause (reproduced first, node harness /tmp/r23-w1-repro.mjs, exit 1): the which-one chip's onClick in `frontend/src/components/RecChat.tsx:527` called `askModel(`Programme id ${p.id}. ${turn.question}`)` and askModel stored that string verbatim as `turn.question`, which the user bubble rendered raw (`<p>{turn.question}</p>`). Tapping "ETH Zurich — 计算机科学理学学士" after "这个专业学费多少？" therefore rendered the bubble as "Programme id eth-cs-bsc-2027. 这个专业学费多少？". Audit of every other chat surface: clean — CompassPet renders only verbatim free text / choice labels and never its constructed messages (suggestMessage / sentMessage with context); PrefChat chips send display labels (country/city names) only; AiEntry never renders its `question` prop, only POSTs it (so the programme-detail page's own `Programme id ${program.id}.` construction is not a leak). Fix (frontend-only, no backend change — the sent marker string is byte-identical, so the backend's programme-id intent keeps matching): the turn splits display from sent. `turn.question` is the user-visible bubble text, `turn.sent` is the exact message POSTed to the backend. New pure helpers in `frontend/src/lib/chat.ts` — `scopedProgrammeMessage(id, q)` = `Programme id <id>. <q>`, `scopedProgrammeDisplay(title, q, locale)` = display name + question (full-width colon in zh locales, ": " elsewhere), `stripProgrammeMarker(text)` = render-side guard stripping a leading marker (anchored, requires the trailing ". ", so a mid-sentence mention is untouched). A segment that was translated AND duplicates the environment label is dropped so the label survives as its own atom (otherwise the atom dedupe drops the shorter label). An unknown phrase is kept only when it is already in the active locale's script family (CJK in zh, Arabic in ar, Latin in en/es/fr/pt-BR); a foreign-script fragment is dropped from the derived summary — it stays stored, and the chat still shows the user's own words. Nothing is machine-translated and nothing is invented: only the three known phrases are recognised. (1) FRONTEND — CompassPet.tsx: `devilMode` is a sticky boolean (state, line ~418): once toggled on it stayed on until manually tapped off, and `onSend` sent `mode='devil'` + the saved profile for EVERY free-text turn while it was on, marking each turn `devil: devilMode` (offline fallback + both reply pushes). Fix: one-shot devil — `onSend` captures `const isDevilTurn = devilMode`, calls `setDevilMode(false)` as the turn is sent (before the fetch, so a question typed while the devil turn is in flight is normal too), and every devil marker in `onSend` uses the per-turn capture; turning devil off before asking restores normal replies at once. The devil turn itself is unchanged (template still ends with the engine-computed difference sentence, e.g. "相差3项。", pinned by test). (2) BACKEND — the normal path could not answer the free question at all: "苏黎世的气候怎么样？" names a climate-table city but no climate class, so `_retrieve` returned nothing and the template answered the blanket unknown, while the devil path (scoped to the profile's countries/climate) always had the conflict line. A non-climate question naming a city gets nothing extra (pinned by test). Also in `_template_result`: a cities/country line replacing the unknown now carries its own evidence link (the climate/country source's URL — previously the replaced line carried no link, since only the prog/scope/rank/sugg branches set `replaced`). (3) HISTORY LEAK — `buildHistory` fed devil turns' lines back as model context for later normal turns; devil turns (`m.devil`) are now excluded, so the devil voice cannot leak into unrelated answers through history. The locale re-fetch effect is unchanged (a devil turn is still re-fetched in devil mode via its own `m.devil` flag). All six ambiguous_programme templates now carry {count} and ask() passes count=len(pids) — count derived from the same list rendered. Behaviour verified: the ETH question now lists ETH's two CS programmes with count "2"; with a level word it answers the verified fee line (CHF 4,380/year); the Chinese ETH fee question resolves directly to the CS BSc fee line; ETH Medicine is the honest unknown; bare "Computer Science" (no institution) still lists the three exact-name programmes but now says "three". | 277 | Round 24 W4 (pet free-text latency, P2). Diagnosis (measured, stub gateways): retrieval 0.06 s, template 0.01 s — the 25–30 s offline wait was 100% the model attempt. The 25–30 s upper end is httpx per-phase timeouts: timeout=15.0 sets connect=read=write=pool=15 s each, so one call can take ~30 s when connect is slow and the read then hangs — not shortened (live calls take 9–12 s), fixed by not attempting when known down. Fix: ProviderError carries status_code (8 raise sites); _trips_breaker() trips on slow 5xx in chat()/achat()/_probe_chat_call(); fast 5xx still doesn't trip, 429/4xx never trip. Frontend: a modelAvailability()==='unavailable' gate was added to sendAskTurn, then REMOVED at integration (see #278) — the backend breaker fix is what makes the down-path fast. askSuggest keeps its gate; drive-by: both gates now clear busy/waking on the early-return path (askSuggest's gate previously left the pet stuck thinking). Tests: 5 new in test_r24_w4_pet_latency.py; after slow 502 once (~12 s, inherent) then 0.00 s / 0.01 s turns, breaker open. Open: frontend caps a turn at 18 s, so a 25–30 s UI wait doesn't match current frontend code — verify against the live deploy. Not attempted: server-side _model_skip_cached parity for /assistant/chat (needs the probe cache moved out of app.main to break the circular import — bigger refactor). |

| 281 | Round 25 W1 (with-profile fee questions slow and inconsistent, task 19 P1). NOTE on numbering: the task brief said #279, but sibling W3 already holds #279 and W2's test file cites #280 for its own entry — this entry takes #281; the coordinator renumbers on integration. Root cause (reproduced first, /tmp/r25w1-repro.py, stub gateways): every /assistant/chat turn with a key configured attempted the model first, and the only fail-fast was the circuit breaker, which opens only AFTER a slow failure and only for 180 s — so a turn's outcome depended on gateway/breaker timing, not the question. BEFORE: fee Q + profile, hanging gateway, fresh breaker 16.4/16.1/16.0 s (3 runs) → template list; same Q, healthy gateway 0.10 s, ai_status=available, NO candidates (model answered directly, list skipped) — the flip; single-match fee Q, hanging gateway, probe seeded "unavailable" 16.07 s (the backend ignored the known-down verdict — /simulate's _model_skip_cached lived in app.main and /assistant/chat could not import it without a circular import); fee Q WITHOUT profile 16.08 s — no profile-dependent branch exists in the send path (the only with-profile difference is the appended saved-preferences context, which retrieval already strips), so the tester's "without profile instant" was breaker-state luck. Fix: (1) Deterministic routing, decided once per turn from the retrieval result before any gateway contact — a template reply carrying candidates in one of two shapes is answered by the template, never by the model: exactly one candidate (the scope already resolved it and the template states its verified fact — the step-7 fee verifications, Round 25 W2) or exactly two candidates, one undergraduate + one taught_masters (a level answer uniquely resolves it — the ETH fee flow). Helper _candidates_route_to_template in ai/chat.py. The narrowing is deliberate and was forced by two pinned behaviours the first (broad: any candidates → template) version broke: "What are Oxford's tuition fees?" matches six programmes across fields/levels, which no level answer can resolve, and a Round 6 test pins the model answering it from verified evidence; and the devil-breaker test's setup (breaker expired, probe cache fresh) is a state unreachable in real time — the 60 s cache always expires 120 s before the 180 s breaker — so that test now ages the cache to match the simulated 180 s (same pattern the neighbouring re-probe test already used). (2) Fail-fast parity with /simulate: the probe cache + skip verdict moved out of app.main into new backend/app/ai_status_cache.py (imports only ai.provider, no cycle); main.py re-exports the names (app.main._AI_STATUS_CACHE is the same dict object, reset_ai_status_cache and _model_skip_cached keep working — the existing tests keep mutating the cache through app.main); ai/chat.py answer() consults model_skip_cached() before the model attempt, devil included. AFTER (same repro): A 0.13/0.08/0.02 s, B 0.08 s with the byte-identical list (no flip), C 0.01 s, D 0.01 s, every E/E-zh step ≤0.08 s with the fee line reached, F still model/available for a single-match question when up; total repro runtime 186 s → ~6 s. Finding (browser re-test task 19, 2026-11-27 17:36–17:45 CST): every step-7 verification showed "正在查已核实资料… 0 秒" and counted up to ~12–14 s before the reply appeared. Diagnosis (measured against the LIVE deployed backend, Render HEAD 263ec20d, minutes after the window, exact step-7 payload — zh-CN fee question, program_ids ["ucl-cs-bsc-2026"], track "undergraduate"): the wait is a LIVE MODEL CALL that succeeds, not a burned timeout, retry, or cold start. 17:49–17:50 CST, gateway down, breaker open: 0.81 / 0.80 / 0.59 s, ai_status unavailable (template). 17:54 CST, cold breaker: call 1 15.60 s ai_status AVAILABLE, model deepseek/deepseek-v4.1-flash (genuine live answer); call 2 16.18 s unavailable (one slow failure, breaker tripped); call 3 0.96 s unavailable (breaker open). 17:58–17:59 CST, cold breaker again: call 1 15.98 s unavailable, calls 2–5 0.57–0.79 s unavailable. The 12–14 s sits inside the live-model range (9–12 s on 2026-11-26; 15.6 s here); a down gateway cannot produce "every check 12–14 s" while the Round 24 W4 breaker works, because every check after the first slow failure lands in ~1 s. Trace: step 7 = RecChat recommend step; each verification is one askModel turn → exactly one POST /api/v1/assistant/chat → ai.chat.answer() → at most one provider.chat() (timeout_s=15.0, no repair retry); frontend fetchJsonChat caps one attempt at 18 s with retryOnTimeout=false, so no retry multiplier; retrieval ~0.06 s, template ~0.01 s (W4). Single uvicorn worker (Dockerfile CMD has no --workers), so the module-level breaker is shared by all requests. Verdict: INHERENT — no behaviour change by W2; shortening the 15 s budget would clip real replies (Round 10), answering template-first would throw away the working model answer. What Round 25 W1 (same round, other worker — in the working copy uncommitted, no DECISIONS entry yet at the time of writing) already cut for this path, measured locally here: a scoped fee question (one programme in scope) is answered by the template at once (candidates route — 0 transport calls, ~0.01 s, verified fee line; W1 narrowed the route during this session to _candidates_route_to_template: exactly 1 candidate, or exactly 2 candidates one per level); a turn while the gateway is known down skips the model entirely (model_skip_cached parity for /assistant/chat, probe cache moved out of app.main into app.ai_status_cache — the bigger refactor DECISIONS #277 left undone). The slow case that remains after W1 is a non-candidate question with a cold cache and a closed breaker while the gateway is UP: one live model call, 12–16 s, inherent — and the first slow failure after the gateway dies is inherent too (the /simulate residual, #267). Tests: 8 new in tests/test_r25_w2.py, all pass standalone and in subsets (with r25_w1, r24_w4, r18_w2); they pin the live-model cost (every turn pays the gateway's time when up), the scoped-fee skip (0 calls, fee line, candidates), the known-down skip (0 calls), the slow-failure-then-fast endpoint shape, and the single-attempt shape on both sides. Full suite: run 1: 766 passed, 4 failed — all four pass in isolation (test_ai_chat Oxford, test_r14 pet-restores, test_r18 devil-after-breaker-expiry, test_r23_w2 clear-nonce regreet); run 2: 770 passed, 2 failed — both W2 scoped-fee tests, also passing in isolation and in subsets. Both runs overlapped live edits of backend/ai/chat.py by the W1 worker (mtimes 10:06:14 during run 1, 10:22:04 during run 2; test_r25_w1.py edited at the same times), so the full-run failures are attributed to concurrent-edit races / intermediate routing states, not to this change — W2 changed no production code. Run 3 re-run on the quiesced tree (no edits since 10:22 UTC): 773 passed, 0 failed (10m55s) — confirming the run-1/run-2 failures were concurrent-edit races. (Entry amended after run 3.) Open (documented, NOT fixed): a slow failure mode that never trips the breaker would still repeat per check — slow HTTP 200 with an unreadable body (kind "bad_reply"), slow empty reply (kind "empty_reply", raised after the trip block in provider.chat/achat), or a slow 200 whose text fails model-JSON parsing / validation in ai.chat (answer() falls back and chat() already cleared the breaker on HTTP success). | 285 | Round 26 W3 (same fee question flips to a rankings line on the 5th ask, re-test 20). Root cause (proven by repro, see ~/workspace/r26-w3-report.md): not a template or frontend bug — the template cannot produce the rankings line for a fee question (no rank word), so it came from the model path. With a UG profile the question matches a single programme, so Round 25 routing does not apply and the turn is model-first whenever the gateway allows it; _retrieve()'s scope loop put ETH's QS/THE/ARWU rank sources into the evidence catalogue alongside the fee facts; neither the prompt nor validation pinned the answer type to the question's intent (a rankings-line payload validated with zero errors). The template is history-invariant; only the model route can flip, and the exact trigger on turn 5 is unprovable (no model transcript by design) — the patch removes the possibility, not the trigger. Fix: _retrieve() gates the scope loop's ranking_sources() on rank intent; answer() runs a deterministic post-validation intent check (_model_lines_match_intent, fee intent only, devil excluded) requiring the fee figure in the rendered lines, else the template fallback; chat_system prompt hardened (advisory only). Integration adjustments: the intent check compares the fee FIGURE (digits, commas stripped), not the full fee_text string — the qualifier suffix ('GBP 48,600 per year (overseas)') wrongly rejected grounded model lines (5 test_ai_chat failures); and test_r21_w3_why_scope::test_scope_sources_join_catalogue's rank-source assertion is superseded by Layer 1 by design (updated to assert absence; the programme-source pin is unchanged). Tests: tests/test_r26_w3_fee_intent.py, 5 new, all pass (fee question 5x against a rankings-line stub answers the fee every time). Residual: Layer 2 covers fee intent only; IELTS/deadline/rank can follow once the fee pin is proven in re-test. | The model could answer a fee question with ranks because rank evidence was always in the catalogue and nothing pinned answer type to intent; the fix gates the evidence and checks the output deterministically. |

| 302 | Round 29 E4: guided pref answers deterministic, no model call (re-test 23 item c) | Root cause: guided pref turns paid a model call whenever the gateway was not already known down, and the gateway is slow (13-30 s for the countries answer). | 300 | Round 29 E3-frontend: cities-line scoping in the PrefChat render (re-test 23 item b) | Root cause, verified in code: the backend places the cities line whenever climate is the next question as well as when the turn answers climate, so the countries turn AND the climate turn both carried it (re-test 23 round 1). Dedupe is by marker, NOT server-side removal — the cities line is still placed on the countries turn, so it can still appear in `lines` on two consecutive turns; the frontend renders it only on the turn where `climate_turn` is True. #305 records Worker 2's marker plumbing (`PrefChatReply` / `PrefFallbackReplyLike` gain `climate_turn?: boolean`; PrefChat mapping `answeredClimate: turn.reply?.climate_turn ?? !!turn.reply?.extracted?.climate`, falling back to the extraction for old persisted snapshots). This entry records Worker 1's consuming side. Backend lines are authoritative; the render dedupes identical lines within a turn (`dedupeTemplateLines`, #298) and never duplicates sentences the backend already appended. Also fixed Worker 2's one-line syntax typo in PrefChat.tsx line 1748 (a `/* */`-style continuation without `//`, tsc TS1005) — the only change made to Worker 2's text. Tests: tests/test_r29_w1.py E3 cases (pure `scopeCitiesLines`; a composed two-turn zh conversation rendered in en with the cities line once on the climate turn; `climate_turn` marker case; model turn untouched) + tests/test_r29_w3_climate_turn.py (Worker 2, #305). Verification: wide related-files run 150 passed, 2 failed — both failures are other workers' documented intentional breaks, not this worker's: (1) tests/test_r17_w1.py::test_r17_w1_source_pins counts a 4th `setPendingExtract(null)` site — that site is Worker 3's E6 clear effect at PrefChat.tsx:703 (#304, intentional; the coordinator reconciles the pin 3 → 4). Correction to #302's attribution: #302 said the 4th site came "from the parallel frontend worker (Worker 1, E2)" — incorrect; this worker's E2 work added no `setPendingExtract(null)` site (verified: the four sites are 703 clear effect, 1017 confirmExtract, 1047 dismissExtract, 1589 restart). | 307 | Round 30 F1: unknown pair dropped on a guided turn whose answer was recorded (re-test 24 item a + re-test 25) | Root cause, verified in code and by reproduction before the fix: the offline template comes from `assistant_ask` (backend/ai/assistant.py `ask`, line 1392), which knows nothing about the pref-chat extraction — a message matching no fee/IELTS/deadline/rank/country question gets the generic `_T` "unknown" + "unknown_2" pair — and `_fallback_result` (backend/ai/pref_chat.py) copied `template["lines"][:2]` verbatim, replacing lines[0] only with a cities/country line when one applied. Reproduced: `pref_answer("大学城", "zh-CN", known={"countries": ["CH"], "climate": "cool"})` returned `extracted == {"environment": "campus"}` AND lines `["这个我从已核实的数据里查不到，不知道。", "可以开始资格检查，逐项核对你的背景。", "有没有你心仪的城市？"]`. Fix, backend only: in `_fallback_result`, `had_unknown_pair` is captured before the cities/country replacement (the replacement can leave `unknown_2` behind on its own, a climate turn's [cities, unknown_2]), and when this turn's deterministic extraction recorded a preference (`extracted` non-empty) AND the template actually carried the pair, both pair lines are dropped. Only the pair is dropped, and only when present: a cities/country line stays, a specific unknown (`fee_unknown`, `ask_ielts_unknown`, …) is never a member of the pair and stays, and when the extraction genuinely failed (`extracted == {}`) the pair stays exactly as before — that is the honest "I don't know" case. Layer choice: backend, not the frontend `prefFallbackLines.ts` — the pair is emitted by the backend template, the frontend can only re-localise or hide stored lines, and suppressing there would leave the false claim in the API response (and in every other consumer); the R29 E1 re-localisation of stored unknown-pair lines is untouched (no frontend file changed, no i18n keys added/changed, no copy/data changed). A guided turn now answers with the question line alone; a WHY turn with no lines at all (the WHY is the turn's question) while the cities evidence line stays (Round 7: evidence already in hand); a done teaching turn answers with `lines == []` — the frontend already renders a turn with empty reply lines as its offline line + question bubble (`prefTurnBubbles` emits the question bubble "even with empty lines"). New tests/test_r30_f1_unknown_pair.py, 14 tests: (a) the six re-test guidance turns (environment/city/afterGrad/salary CHF/pressure/teaching, in wizard order) plus the exact curl-verified environment case — extracted as expected, no unknown-pair line in any of the 6 locales, reply is the next question alone (empty on the done turn); (b) genuinely failed extraction still emits the pair, zh + en + mid-flow (a free question while city is asked); (c) climate turn with extraction drops the leftover `unknown_2` behind the cities line, WHY turn keeps the cities line only, country question with extraction keeps its country line verbatim, and a mock-model turn whose own narration IS the unknown line returns it verbatim with `ai_status == "available"` (model path untouched — the F1 filter lives in the offline fallback only; R29 E1 model veto). Targeted run: `.venv/bin/pytest tests/test_r30_f1_unknown_pair.py -q -p no:cacheprovider` → 14 passed. No TS edited, so no `tsc` run (not applicable); full suite + build left to the coordinator. No push/deploy. Residuals: a guided answer the deterministic extraction cannot read still takes the model path and can fall back to the pair honestly (extraction failed); the pet free-chat path (`ai/chat.py` line 2442, same `assistant_ask` template) is untouched — the same pair there is a separate path, out of scope for this defect. |
| 308 | Round 30 F2: wizard summary reads climate first instead of question order (re-test 24 item a) | Root cause, verified in code: the summary is `prefSummary` in frontend/src/lib/preferences.ts (lines 79-100 before the fix) — NOT PrefChat.tsx or lib/chat.ts (those only call it; `extractedSummary` in chat.ts already builds in question order). `prefSummary` pushed its parts in field order — climate, countries, note, environment, city, afterGrad, salary, pressure, teaching — so the re-test 24 summary read "凉爽 · 瑞士 · 环境优美 · 大学城·校园氛围 · 苏黎世 · 留在留学国家 · 80000 CHF · 适中 · 小班研讨" (climate before the country it qualifies). The pushes were written in field order, not question order; no map/set iteration was involved. Fix, frontend only, render/composition time only (stored Profile shape, dedupe and localisation untouched; no new i18n keys, no copy/data change): the part-building is extracted into the exported pure helper `prefSummaryParts(profile, t, locale?)` in the same file, which pushes in the canonical question order — countries, reason (the countries note), climate, environment, city, afterGrad, salary, pressure, teaching — and `prefSummary` now composes from it (`const parts = prefSummaryParts(profile, t, locale);`); the atom-splitting, containment dedupe and note '·'-segment strip below are unchanged. The fix flows to all four summary surfaces (results page, report page, PrefChat step 8, pet) since they all call `prefSummary`. New tests/test_r30_f2_summary_order.py, 2 tests (node harness over the compiled `lib/preferences.ts`, same pattern as test_r13_pref_summary.py — the TS ordering is covered by a Python test that compiles the TS with tsc and executes the real `prefSummary`/`prefSummaryParts` in node with fixed profiles and asserts exact output): the re-test 24 profile in exact canonical order in zh-CN ("瑞士 · 环境优美 · 凉爽 · 大学城·校园氛围 · 苏黎世 · 留在留学国家 · 80000 CHF · 适中 · 小班研讨") and en ("Switzerland · Beautiful environment · Cool · University town · campus vibe · Zurich · Stay in the country where I study · 80000 CHF · Moderate · Small seminars"), the helper's part texts in exactly canonical order in both locales plus its `fromNote` tags, partial-profile order pins (countries before climate; salary/pressure/teaching order), and a no-mutation check on the profile; plus a Python source pin that `prefSummary` delegates to `prefSummaryParts`, the helper's pushes are in canonical order, and `prefSummary` itself builds no parts. Five existing test files pinned the old climate-first order in their expected `prefSummary` strings and were updated to the canonical order (expectations only, no logic): tests/test_r13_pref_summary.py (audit + multi-country), tests/test_r14_pref_chat.py (audit), tests/test_r17_w1.py (both summary strings), tests/test_r23_w3_pref_summary_locale.py (`_AUDIT_WANT`, all 6 locales), tests/test_r27_w4.py (`_AUDIT_WANT` + 3 `_PREF_CASES` strings). Deliberately NOT updated: the historical docstring quotes of the old order in test_r13/test_r23_w3 (they describe the original defects), and tests/test_r28_d1.py / tests/test_r29_w1.py, whose "Cool · Switzerland" strings are `localisedConfirmedNote` (chat.ts) inputs/outputs, not `prefSummary` — that path preserves stored-note order by design and is untouched. Verification: targeted run `.venv/bin/pytest tests/test_r30_f2_summary_order.py tests/test_r13_pref_summary.py tests/test_r14_pref_chat.py tests/test_r17_w1.py tests/test_r23_w3_pref_summary_locale.py tests/test_r27_w4.py -q` — 14 passed (the new test failed once during development on a wrong expectation of mine: `joinList` joins countries with " · " in the zh-CN locale, not ", " — the expectation was corrected, the code was right); `npx tsc --noEmit` in frontend/ clean. No push/deploy. Residuals: live-browser re-test still owed (subagent cannot use the browser); the `localisedConfirmedNote` path (confirmed-note bubbles) keeps whatever order the stored note was built in — by design, since it re-localises a stored string rather than composing from the profile. |
| 309 | Round 30 F3: pet footer sits on "Checking the AI connection…" for ~30–60 s after opening/switching, before settling to offline, even when the backend was already warm (re-test 24 item d + re-test 25 item d) | Root cause — three cooperating defects in the shared verdict path, all frontend, E5 design preserved. (1) Two live probes per open. `useAiVerdict`'s auto-probe effect is registered before CompassPet's open effect (aiVerdict.ts `useAiVerdict` called at CompassPet.tsx:458; open effect at :773), so by the time the open effect ran it had already started the open's probe — and the open effect then called `resetAiVerdict()`, which nulls `inFlight` WITHOUT aborting the first probe's fetch, and started a second one. Both ran server-side, serialised on the backend's `/ai/status` lock (`backend/app/main.py` `with _ai_status_lock:` held across `probe_live()`). Measured locally against a stub gateway in the Round 16 W3 flapping state (list endpoint answers, chat hangs): one live probe 7.13 s (`probed:true`, unavailable, trips the 180 s breaker), a concurrent second probe 7.12 s waiting on the lock (`probed:false`), breaker-open probe 0.003 s, warm non-fresh read 0.002 s; live Render backend: health 0.69 s (warm), breaker-open fresh probe 0.55–0.98 s (`probed:false`, cache_age 67 s). So every open paid two probe durations instead of one. (2) A slow probe attempt was retried. `probeAiVerdict` called `fetchJsonPatient` with the defaults — 25 s per-attempt timeout, retry on a slow abort, 90 s budget (`frontend/src/lib/api.ts` `fetchJsonPatient`, `retryOnTimeout` default true) — so a probe delayed past 25 s by the lock queue was aborted and silently re-issued, re-queueing behind the still-running probes and stacking 25 s aborts. The `useAiVerdict` auto-probe passes no `onRetry`, so no waking line showed: the footer kept showing "Checking…" through the retries. (3) A dropped probe verdict stranded the footer on checking. The Round 22 turn guard (`probeVerdictApplies`) drops a probe verdict while a turn is in flight — which is every open with a saved profile, because `greet()` auto-starts a turn before the open effect's probe resolves — and `probeAiVerdict` then resolved to the unchanged 'checking' state with no probe in flight and nothing scheduled to re-probe (`useAiVerdict` only fires on a verdict change). The footer depended entirely on the turn's verdict, and a turn ending WITHOUT noting one (superseded by a restart) left it stranded until the next explicit probe. Fix, frontend-only. `frontend/src/lib/aiVerdict.ts`: probe fetch `retryOnTimeout: false` (the fetchJsonChat rule from Round 15 F2 — a status read is not a turn; a fast failure, i.e. backend waking, still retries inside the 90 s budget) plus an `attemptTimeoutMs` passthrough on `ProbeAiOptions` (same pattern as `budgetMs`); a slow abort settles offline with `timedOut` (AbortError); `endAiTurn()` re-arms a fresh probe when the verdict is still 'checking' and none is in flight (no-op on the normal path, where the turn already noted a verdict). `frontend/src/components/CompassPet.tsx`: the open, /results-restore and devil-enable effects reset the shared verdict only when it is SETTLED (`getAiVerdict().verdict !== 'checking'`) — every open already resets before those effects when there is a stale verdict to clear (toggle pre-paint, restore before setOpen), and on a fresh mount the verdict cannot be settled yet — so each effect's `runProbe` joins the probe `useAiVerdict` already started instead of orphaning it and starting a second live probe. The clear-data reset stays unconditional (pre-clear evidence must be invalidated) and toggle(true) still resets pre-paint, so the R20/R21/R25-W3 guarantees are unchanged; no i18n keys, no copy, no data, no backend change. New tests/test_r30_f3_footer_settle.py, 1 test (node harness over the real lib/aiVerdict.ts compiled with tsc, stubbed fetch, same pattern as tests/test_r29_w3.py): a hanging probe aborts once and settles offline+timedOut (exactly one fetch, ~1 s — verified it fails pre-fix: ~5 s, timedOut false); fast failures still retry (3 fetches, ~4 s — the backend-wake path survives); a dropped probe verdict + a turn ending without a verdict re-arms exactly one probe that settles the footer offline (verified it fails pre-fix: no re-arm, footer stays checking); no re-arm when the turn already noted a verdict; source pins for the three guarded resets, toggle pre-paint reset, unconditional clear-data reset, `retryOnTimeout: false`, the endAiTurn re-arm, and the kept auto-probe. The new harness fails on the pre-fix aiVerdict.ts with 6 failures and passes on the fixed code. Accommodation in tests/test_r29_w3.py §11 only: one extra scripted response for the re-arm probe that `endAiTurn()` now starts (invalidated by the following `resetAiVerdict()` before it can land); no expectation changed. Verification: targeted runs only — new test 1 passed; tests/test_r29_w3.py 1 passed; `npx tsc --noEmit` clean. No push/deploy (coordinator). Residuals: with a saved profile the open probe's verdict is still dropped by design while the auto-turn runs (R22 guard kept), so the footer settles on the turn's verdict — now bounded by the turn's own ~20 s cap plus a fast status read (single probe refreshes the cache / trips the breaker), not 30–60 s; under heavy cross-tab lock contention a probe can still take the full 25 s attempt timeout before settling offline+timedOut (the footer retry then re-checks); reopening the panel while a probe is in flight still orphans that probe's server-side run (toggle's pre-paint reset is load-bearing there) — rare, one probe's ~7 s. Live-browser re-test still owed (subagent cannot use the browser). |
| 310 | Round 30 F4: Arabic hero title letters overlap/stick together (re-test 25 item 7, main visual defect of the restyle) | Root cause: `.display-hero` in frontend/src/app/globals.css carried `font-style: oblique 6deg` (the Xiaohongshu template's slanted display type). Space Grotesk is Latin-only, so Arabic falls back to the system font — but the synthetic oblique still applies to the Arabic glyphs and breaks their cursive joining, so letters collide. Chinese tolerates oblique (verified fine in re-test 25) but Arabic does not, and the html lang is statically "en", so no `:lang(ar)` scoping can distinguish scripts. Fix: the oblique is dropped from `.display-hero` entirely (size, weight, tracking, glow kept); the slant was a minor nod to the video, the Arabic breakage is a real defect. Verified: tsc clean, build 12/12. |
| 311 | Round 30 F5: stats-strip label reads "Language" (singular) in English (re-test 25 item 4) | The strip reused the existing `common.language` key ("Language"). Fix: new key `common.languages` in all 6 locales (en "Languages", zh-CN "语言", es "Idiomas", fr "Langues", pt-BR "Idiomas", ar "اللغات"), used by the stats strip in frontend/src/app/page.tsx; the key was inserted as a single line after `common.language` in each messages file (no wholesale reformat — the files are not fully key-sorted, so a sort_keys rewrite would have churned ~980 lines each). Locale count 980 → 981; the three 980 pins bumped (tests/test_r28_d1.py, tests/test_r29_w1.py, tests/test_r29_w3.py) plus two more found by the Round 30 coordinator (tests/test_r19_w3_duration.py, tests/test_r29_w3_climate_turn.py). Mirrors regenerated, locale tests pass. |
| 312 | Round 31 G1: wizard Q6 (afterGrad) stalls 25-27 s on the second run (re-test 26) | Root cause, verified in code and by reproduction before the fix: the English afterGrad Stay chip label is "Stay in the country where I study", and the backend question detector (`_det_looks_like_question`, backend/ai/pref_chat.py) matched its English question words ANYWHERE in the message — so "where" inside the relative clause ("the country where I study") read as a question. The deterministic extraction read the answer correctly (`{'afterGrad': 'stay'}`), but the Round 29 E4 guided gate (`guided = nxt_missing in det and not _det_looks_like_question(msg)`) was False, so the turn took the model path instead of the deterministic route and, with the gateway hanging, burned the full 15 s backend model budget (`timeout_s=15.0`) inside the frontend's 18 s attempt window. Reproduced pre-fix with a counting mock provider raising `httpx.ReadTimeout`: `pref_answer("Stay in the country where I study", "en", known={countries, climate, environment, city})` made 1 model call. The first run was instant because the breaker (180 s) / probe cache (60 s) was still warm from an earlier slow failure, so `model_skip_cached()` skipped the attempt; the second run's Q6 was the first model-path turn after the caches cooled — and Q1-Q5 were instant because no other chip label contains a question word mid-sentence. The frontend's `looksLikeQuestion` (frontend/src/lib/chat.ts) had the identical unanchored regex, so the same sentence typed as guided free text went to `askModel` instead of `/profile/parse` + the local mapping. The "Checking verified sources…" spinner is the `r9chat.thinkingElapsed` line; the input is disabled while `thinking` is true, so the whole model budget froze the UI. Fix, no copy/data/i18n change, no new key, E4 route and F1/F2/F3 behaviour untouched: the English question words only mark a question when they OPEN the message — `match` instead of `search` in `_det_looks_like_question`, and a `^` anchor on `QUESTION_WORDS_EN` in `looksLikeQuestion`. A genuine question still opens with its question word ("What is the fee?", "Where should I study?") or carries a "?" / CJK marker anywhere (those still match anywhere), so those still take the model path. New tests/test_r31_g1_wizard_stall.py, 22 tests: (a) every locale's afterGrad Stay chip label (read from frontend/messages/*.json) is not a question, and every en chip label for the 8 preferences is not a question; the guided Stay turn in en + zh-CN makes ZERO model calls even against a hanging gateway (one test uses a handler that sleeps 3 s then raises — pre-fix the turn pays the hang; post-fix it never touches the handler and returns in engine time, < 2 s), extracted `{afterGrad: stay}`, next_question salary; (b) genuine questions still attempt the model exactly once, including a question that also matches an enum synonym ("What does take it easy mean?"), a "?"-marked Stay sentence, and a CJK-marked question; (c) node harness over the real lib/chat.ts (compiled with tsc, same pattern as test_r30_f3_footer_settle.py): the frontend detector agrees on the Stay sentence (false), a "?"-marked Stay sentence (true), and opening question words (true), plus a source pin for the `^` anchor. Verification: new test 22 passed; pre-fix behaviour confirmed via a scratch repro (1 model call, detector True); related suites 102 passed (test_r30_f1, test_r29_w1/w2/w3, test_r29_w3_climate_turn, test_r26_w2, test_r25_w1, test_r24_w1, test_r30_f3, test_r30_f2, test_r14_pref_chat, test_r17_w1, test_r23_w3, test_r27_w4, test_r28_d1, test_r13_pref_summary); `npx tsc --noEmit` clean; `npm run build` clean, 12/12 (two incremental builds first failed at the final "Collecting build traces" step with ENOENT on unrelated files — a stale `.next` directory, not the change; a clean rebuild after `rm -rf .next` passed). No push/deploy. Residuals: (1) a question word mid-clause without punctuation ("tell me what take it easy means") can now read as an answer — the confirm bubble is the safety net (nothing is applied until the user confirms the extraction); (2) the deterministic synonym tables are en/zh-only, so the es/fr/pt-BR/ar Stay labels are correctly not questions but still unreadable without a model and take the model path — teaching the extractor the other four locales' labels is separate work (pinned by a test); (3) a guided answer the deterministic extraction cannot read still takes the model path by design and can take up to the ~18 s frontend attempt bound with the input disabled — the E4 route only covers readable answers; (4) the separate re-test 26 observation that /simulate takes ~15 s is NOT covered by this fix and stays a known cold-start residual: /simulate already has the `model_skip_cached()` fail-fast gate (backend/app/main.py:1257) and legitimately wants the model, so its first attempt after a cold breaker/probe cache still pays one 15 s budget before the breaker trips for 180 s. Live-browser re-test still owed (subagent cannot use the browser). |

| 315 | Round 31 G4: pet footer claims "AI answers" while the panel claims offline (re-test 26) | Root cause, verified in code: the two lines read DIFFERENT sources. The stored turn.offline flag still selects the CONTENT (template/rank/directory/catalogue lines) — history preserved, only the state claim moved; a live model reply never gains the lead (turn.offline is false for it); the devil boundary is untouched — it labels the reply's provenance, pinned by Round 18 W3 (devilOffline: turn.devil && turn.offline). | 321 | Round 32 H3: pet old conversation does not clear on sample-profile load (re-test 28) | Root cause, verified in code: the Round 23 W2 / Round 29 E6 clear-data reset works through `dataClearNonce` — `clearData` bumps it (store.tsx:131-155) and CompassPet watches it and resets messages / answers / step, devil off, re-greets. Loading a sample goes through `setProfile` instead (homepage page.tsx:55 `loadSample`, wizard page.tsx:263 `applySample`), and `setProfile` (store.tsx:118) never bumped any nonce — so the pet never heard about the replace and kept the old bubbles ("Undergraduate", "Switzerland") while the profile card showed the sample's (missing) preferences. Same for an own-file import (wizard/page.tsx:283), which also goes through `setProfile`. Fix, frontend-only, no copy / data / route / i18n change: `setProfile` bumps a new `profileReplaceNonce` (store.tsx — declared on AppState, held in state, exposed in the context value + useMemo deps), and CompassPet gains a second reset effect watching it — same reset body as the clear-data effect (messages / answers / step cleared, devil off, shared verdict reset via resetAiVerdict(), re-greet + fresh probe while open), its own ref seeded at mount. A SEPARATE nonce, not the existing `dataClearNonce`: the wizard also subscribes to `dataClearNonce` and resets its step to 0, which would fight the wizard sample load's own `setStep(7)` (the effect runs after the commit and would win). A separate EFFECT, not a second dep on the clear-data effect: the Round 23 W2 / Round 25 W3 / Round 30 F3 pins slice the clear effect by its `}, [dataClearNonce]);` close, and merging the deps would break all three — the clear effect is left byte-identical. The reset fires on the actual load, after the Round 31 G2 confirm dialog: the dialogs only call `loadSample` / `applySample` from `onConfirm` (page.tsx:322, wizard/page.tsx:687); opening the dialog only sets the pending flag. `updateProfile` (patch edits) bumps neither nonce, so an edit mid-chat keeps the conversation; `clearData` does not bump the new nonce (it uses `setProfileState` directly and already bumps `dataClearNonce`), so a clear still resets exactly once. PrefChat needs no change: its stored snapshot restores only on a fingerprint match with the current profile (`parsePrefChatState`, lib/chat.ts), so a stale pref-chat blob is discarded on replace by construction — unlike the pet, whose conversation is in-memory only. Tests: new tests/test_r32_h3_pet_clear_on_sample.py, 7 tests — node harness replaying the nonce sequences against the real reset-effect source (sample load resets + re-greets when open, patch edit does not reset, clear-data still resets, closed panel resets without greeting early, second sample load resets again) + source pins (store exposes the nonce, pet subscribes, wizard does not watch it, reset fires on actual load not dialog open, clear-data path unchanged, only CompassPet subscribes); pins fail on pre-fix code (the `profileReplaceNonce` identifier does not exist pre-fix, verified on stripped copies). One intentional pin update in tests/test_r23_w2_pet_clear_data.py: the exact-destructure pin now carries `profileReplaceNonce` (the clear-nonce subscription itself is unchanged). Verification: 39 passed across the new test + test_r31_g2_sample_confirm.py, test_r31_g3_sample_banner.py, test_restyle2_g3_homepage_banner.py, test_r25_w3_footer_clear_data.py, test_r23_w2_pet_clear_data.py, test_r30_f3_footer_settle.py, test_r18_w1.py, test_r29_w3.py; `npx tsc --noEmit` clean (one transient TS18048 at CompassPet.tsx:1460 seen mid-run was the parallel Round 32 H2 worker's in-progress edit, gone on re-run — not this change).
